You find that, when the ten tribes were exiled, Benjamin and Judah were not exiled, and the ten tribes were saying, Because they are the dwellers of His palace, He did not exile them. There is favoritism in this matter. God forbid, there is no favoritism, but their measure of sin was not yet full. As soon as they sinned, they were exiled. Then the ten tribes were bewildered, without an answer in their mouths, and they said, “Behold God! Behold the Mighty One! Behold the Just One! For even to those who dwell in His house He shows no favoritism.” This is to fulfill what is stated: Among the tribes of Israel I made known a true judgment. [from Lam. Rabbah, Proem 6]



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Hosea 5:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:10:1)


**כְּמַסִּיגֵי גְבוּל.** כְּאָדָם הַמַּדְבִּיק גְּבוּל חֲבֵרוֹ, כָּךְ הֵם מִהֲרוּ לֶאֱחוֹז בְּדַרְכֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל חֲבֵרֵיהֶם הַמְּלָכִים. לְפִיכָךְ עֲלֵיהֶם אֶשְׁפּוֹךְ וְגוֹמֵר. וּכְמַשְׁמָעוֹ הָיוּ גּוֹזְלִים שָׂדוֹת, אַךְ קָשֶׁה בְּעֵינַי כִּי הָיָה לוֹ לִכְתּוֹב מַסִּיגֵי גְבוּל וְלֹא כְּמַסִּיגֵי:


**like those who remove the landmark** As a person who attaches the landmark of his neighbor, so did they hasten to grasp the ways of Israel, their colleagues, the kings. Therefore, “upon them will I pour My wrath like water.” And, according to its apparent meaning, they were stealing fields. But it is difficult to me, since he should have written. מַסִיגֵי גְבוּל, those who remove the landmark, not כְּמַסִיגֵי like those who remove.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Hosea 5:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:11:1)


**עָשׁוּק אֶפְרַיִם** בְּיַד הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת:


**Ephraim is plundered** in the hands of the nations.


###### Rashi on Hosea 5:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:11:2)


**רְצוּץ מִשְׁפָּט.** מְיֻסָּר בְּיִסּוּרִים, וְלָמָּה לוֹ כָּל זֹאת? **כִּי הוֹאִיל** וְרָצָה וְהָלַךְ אַחֲרֵי צַוָּאוֹת חֲדָשׁוֹת שֶׁל נְבִיאֵי הַבַּעַל:


**broken by judgment** Chastised with torments. Now why does he suffer with all this? Because he wished and desired and followed the new commands of the prophets of Baal.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Hosea 5:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:12:1)


**וְכָרָקָב.** תּוֹלַעַת הָאוֹכֵל הָעֵץ וְשׁוֹחֲקוֹ:


**and like decay** A worm which eats the wood and pulverizes it.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Hosea 5:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:13:1)


**מְזוֹרוֹ.** לְשׁוֹן ׳חֹלִי׳:


**his pain** Heb. מְזֹרוֹ, an expression of illness.


###### Rashi on Hosea 5:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:13:2)


**וַיֵּלֶךְ אֶפְרַיִם אֶל אַשּׁוּר.** זֶה הוֹשֵׁעַ בֶּן אֵלָה, שֶׁהָיָה לוֹ לְעֶבֶד וְיָשַׁב וַיִּמְרֹד (מלכים ב יז:א; מלכים ב כד:א):


**and Ephraim went to Assyria** This is Hosea son of Elah, who was his vassal and then rebelled against him (II Kings 17:4).


###### Rashi on Hosea 5:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:13:3)


**וַיִּשְׁלַח.** יְהוּדָה:


**and he sent** i.e., Judah sent.


###### Rashi on Hosea 5:13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:13:4)


**אֶל מֶלֶךְ יָרֵב.** זֶה אָחָז, שֶׁנָּתַן שֹׁחַד לְתִגְלַת פִּלְאֶסֶר לְעָזְרוֹ עַל רְצִין מֶלֶךְ אֲרָם וּפֶקַח בֶּן רְמַלְיָה (מלכים ב טז:ז-ח):


**to the king of Yarev** This refers to Ahaz, who gave a bribe to Tiglath-pileser to assist him against Rezin the king of Aram and Pekah son of Remaliah (II Kings 16:8).


###### Rashi on Hosea 5:13:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:13:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:13:5)


**וְהוּא לֹא יוּכַל לִרְפּוֹא לָכֶם.** מֵהַרְבֵּה גְּדוּדִים מִפְּלִשְׁתִּים וַעֲרָבִיִּים שֶׁבָּאוּ עַל אָחָז, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ בְּדִבְרֵי הַיָּמִים (דברי הימים ב כח:כא): ״כִּי חָלַק אָחָז אֶת בֵּית ה׳ וַיִּתֵּן לְמֶלֶךְ אַשּׁוּר וְלֹא לְעֶזְרָה לוֹ״:


**but he will not be able to heal you** from the many bands of Philistines and Arabs that attacked Ahaz, as is related in Chronicles (II 28:21): “For Ahaz divided the house of the Lord etc. and he gave it to the king of Assyria, but he did not help him.”


###### Rashi on Hosea 5:13:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:13:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:13:6)


**וְלֹא יִגְהֶה מִכֶּם מָזוֹר.** לֹא יָסוּר מִכֶּם מַכְאוֹב, וְאוֹמֵר אֲנִי שֶׁהוּא מִן הַתֵּבוֹת הַהֲפוּכוֹת, לְשׁוֹן ״כַּאֲשֶׁר הוֹגָה מִן הַמְּסִלָּה״ (שמואל ב כ:יג), הוֹרוּ וְהוֹגוֹ מִלֵּב״ (ישעיהו נט:יג), ״הָגוֹ סִיגִים מִכָּסֶף״ (משלי כה:ד):


**and he will not remove the pain from you** Heb. וְלֹא יִגְהֶה מִכֶּם מָזוֹר. And I say that it is one of the words whose radicals are transposed. This is an expression similar to (II Sam. 20:13) “When he was removed (הוֹגָה) from the highway”; (Isaiah 59:13) “sprouting and giving forth (והוֹגוֹ) from the heart”; (Prov. 25:4), “take away (הָגוֹ) the dross from the silver.”



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Hosea 5:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:14:1)


**אֶשָּׂא וְאֵין מַצִּיל. אֶשָּׂא** אֶת הַטֶּרֶף:


**I will carry off and no one will rescue** I will carry off the prey.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Hosea 5:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:15:1)


**אֵלֵךְ.** אֶסְתַּלֵּק מֵהֶם וְאָשׁוּבָה אֶל הַשָּׁמַיִם:


**I will go away** I will withdraw from them and return to the heaven.


###### Rashi on Hosea 5:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:15:2)


**עַד אֲשֶׁר יֶאְשְׁמוּ.** יַחֲזִיקוּ עַצְמָם כַּאֲשֵׁמִים לְהוֹדוֹת עַל אַשְׁמָתָם (ויקרא ה:ה):


**until they admit their guilt** i. e., until they consider themselves guilty, to confess their guilt.


###### Rashi on Hosea 5:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/5:15:3)


**יְשַׁחֲרֻנְנִי.** יְבַקְשׁוּנְנִי:


**they will seek Me** Heb. יִשַׁחֲרֻנְנִי.



## Chapter 6


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Hosea 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:1:1)


**לְכוּ וְנָשׁוּבָה.** יֹאמְרוּ: ״לְכוּ וְנָשׁוּבָה וְגוֹ׳״:


**Come and let us return** They will say, “Come and let us return etc.”


###### Rashi on Hosea 6:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:1:2)


**יַךְ וְיַחְבְּשֵׁנוּ.** יַךְ לְשׁוֹן הוֹוֶה הוּא, הוּא מַכֶּה אוֹתָנוּ וְהוּא **יַחְבְּשֵׁנוּ**:


**He smites, and He will bind us up** Heb. יַךְ. It is a present tense. He smites us, and He will bind us up.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Hosea 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:2:1)


**יְחַיֵּנוּ מִיּוֹמַיִם.** יְחַזְּקֵנוּ מִשְּׁתֵּי פֻּרְעָנוּת שֶׁעָבְרוּ עָלֵינוּ מִשְּׁתֵּי מִקְדָּשׁוֹת שֶׁחָרְבוּ:


**He will revive us from the two days** He will strengthen us from the two retributions which have passed over us from the two sanctuaries that were destroyed.


###### Rashi on Hosea 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:2:2)


**בַּיּוֹם הַשְּׁלִישִׁי.** בְּבִנְיַן בַּיִת הַשְּׁלִישִׁי יְקִימֵנוּ:


**on the third day** With the construction of the third Temple, He will set us up.


###### Rashi on Hosea 6:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:2:3)


**מִיּוֹמָיִם.** מִשְּׁתֵּי עִתִּים שֶׁעָבְרוּ עָלֵינוּ:


**from the two days** From the two times that have passed over us.


###### Rashi on Hosea 6:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:2:4)


**בַּיּוֹם הַשְּׁלִישִׁי.** בָּעֵת הַשְּׁלִישִׁי:


**on the third day** In the third time.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Hosea 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:3:1)


**לָדַעַת אֶת ה׳.** בֶּאֱמֶת וּבְתָמִים נְכוֹנִים, כְּשַׁחַר זֶה אֲשֶׁר נָכוֹן מוֹצָאוֹ:


**to know the Lord** truly and wholeheartedly, as sure as the dawn, whose going forth is sure.


###### Rashi on Hosea 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:3:2)


**וְיָבוֹא כַגֶּשֶׁם לָנוּ.** כַּגֶּשֶׁם הַבָּא לִהְיוֹת חַיִּים לָעוֹלָם, שֶׁאָז יָבֹא לְהוֹרוֹת חַיִּים שֶׁיָבֹאוּ לָנוּ:


**and He will come to us like rain** Like rain which comes to be life for the world, for then He will come to teach life that will come to us.


###### Rashi on Hosea 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:3:3)


**יוֹרֶה אָרֶץ.** יַרְוֶה אָרֶץ:


**which satisfies the earth** Heb. יוֹרֶה אֶרֶץ.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Hosea 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:4:1)


**מָה אֶעֱשֶׂה לְּךָ.** מִפְּנֵי מִדַּת הַדִּין, אֵיךְ אוֹצִיא מִשְׁפָּטֶיךָ לָאוֹר (הושע ו:ה):


**What shall I do for you** because of the Divine Standard of Justice; how can I bring out your verdicts to the light?


###### Rashi on Hosea 6:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:4:2)


**וְחַסְדְּכֶם כְּעֲנַן בֹּקֶר.** כָּל טוֹבַתְכֶם וְצִדְקַתְכֶם אֵינָהּ כְּלוּם:


**For your loving-kindness is like a morning cloud** All your goodness and your righteousness is naught.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Hosea 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:5:1)


**עַל כֵּן חָצַבְתִּי בַּנְּבִיאִים.** עַל אֲשֶׁר חָצַבְתִּי עֲלֵיהֶם דְּבָרַי עַל יְדֵי הַנְּבִיאִים, וְהֵם לֹא נִזְהֲרוּ בָּהֶם, לְפִיכָךְ **הֲרַגְתִּים בְּאִמְרֵי פִי** – בִּשְׁבִיל אִמְרֵי פִי. כָּךְ תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן:


**Because I have hewed by the prophets** Heb. עַל-כֵּן. Because I hewed My words upon them through the prophets, but they did not take heed, therefore... **I have put them to death because of the words of My mouth** Because of the words of My mouth. So did Jonathan translate it.


###### Rashi on Hosea 6:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:5:2)


**עַל כֵּן.** כְּמוֹ ׳עַל אֲשֶׁר׳, וְכֵן ״כִּי עַל כֵּן רָאִיתִי פָנֶיךָ״ (בראשית לג:י), ״כִּי עַל כֵּן לֹא נְתַתִּיהָ לְשֵׁלָה בְנִי״ (שם לח:כו):


**Because** Heb. עַל-כֵּן. Like עַל אֲשֶׁר, because of that which. Comp. (Gen. 33:10) “Because (כִּי עַל-כֵּן) I saw your face”; (ibid. 27:36) “because (כִּי עַל-כֵּן) I did not give her to Shelah my son.”


###### Rashi on Hosea 6:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:5:3)


**וּמִשְׁפָּטֶיךָ אוֹר יֵצֵא.** בִּתְמִיהָה, וְאֵיךְ אוּכַל לָשֵׂאת לָכֶם פָּנִים מִפְּנֵי מִדַּת הַדִּין? וּמוּסָב עַל ״מָה אֶעֱשֶׂה לְךָ״:


**now will your verdicts come out to the light?** This is a question. Now how will I be able to show you favoritism before the Divine Standard of Justice? This is a continuation of “What shall I do for you?”



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Hosea 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:6:1)


**וְדַעַת אֱלֹהִים.** חָפַצְתִּי **מֵעוֹלוֹת**:


**and knowledge of God** I desire more than burnt offerings.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Hosea 6:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:7:1)


**כְּאָדָם.** כְּאָדָם הָרִאשׁוֹן:


**like Adam** Heb. כְּאַדָם, like the first man.


###### Rashi on Hosea 6:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:7:2)


**שָׁם בָּגְדוּ בוֹ.** בְּאֶרֶץ טוֹבָה שֶׁהוֹשַׁבְתִּים בָּהּ, שָׁם בָּגְדוּ בִי, כְּאָדָם שֶׁהִכְנַסְתִּיו לְגַן עֵדֶן וְעָבַר עַל מִצְוָתִי:


**there they betrayed Me** In a good land where I settled them, there they betrayed Me, like Adam, whom I brought into the Garden of Eden, and he transgressed My commandment. [from Gen. Rabbah 19:9]



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Hosea 6:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:8:1)


**עֲקֻבָּה מִדָּם.** מְלֵאָה אוֹרְבֵי רְצִיחָה, כְּמוֹ ״וְאֶת עֹקְבוֹ״ (יהושע ח:יג), ״וַיַּעְקְבֵנִי״ (בראשית כז:לו):


**who lurk to shed blood** Heb. עֲקֻבָּה. Full of people who lurk to murder. Comp. (Jos. 8:13) “and their lyers in wait (עֲקֵבוֹ)”; (Gen. 27:36) “and he deceived me (וַיַּעְקְבֵנִי).”



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Hosea 6:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:9:1)


**וּכְחַכֵּי אִישׁ גְּדוּדִים.** וְכַאֲסִיפַת אִישׁ צַיִד דָּגִים הָאוֹסְפָם בְּחַכָּה יַחַד, כֵּן חַכֵּי גְדוּדֵיהֶם חֲבוּרוֹת כֹּהֲנֵיהֶם הַמִּתְחַבְּרִים לָלֶכֶת בַּדֶּרֶךְ, יְרַצְּחוּ שָׁם כֻּלָּם ״שְׁכֶם אֶחָד״ (בראשית מח:כב):


**And as a man gathers fish, so do bands** Heb. וּכְחַכֵּי. And as a fisherman gathers fish, who gathers them with a fishhook together, so do bands gather. Companies of their priests, who gather to go on the road, will all murder there in one group.


###### Rashi on Hosea 6:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:9:2)


**כִּי זִמָּה עָשׂוּ.** כִּי עֵצָה הִיא אֲשֶׁר נִתְיַעֲצוּ לִפְנֵי אֲסִיפָתָם לְאֵיזֶה מָקוֹם יֵלְכוּ לִרְצֹחַ וְלִשְׁלֹל שָׁלָל. **וּכְחַכֵּי** אימדור״ש בְּלַעַ״ז, וְאַף בִּלְשׁוֹן מִשְׁנָה חַכֵּי לְשׁוֹן ׳חִבּוּר׳ הוּא (בבא קמא קיט,ב), וְלֹא יַטִּיל בּוֹ יוֹתֵר מִשְּׁלֹשָׁה חַכִּין, הֵם תְּפִירוֹת שֶׁהַסּוֹרֵק מְחַבֵּר בָּהֶן שְׁנֵי רָאשִׁין מִן הַבֶּגֶד יַחַד כְּשֶׁחוֹבְטוֹ בְּמַקְלוֹת. וְלָשׁוֹן זֶה מִשֶּׁלִּי וְהָגוּן בְּעֵינַי וְכֵן פֵּרוּשׁוֹ, וּכְשֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהֶם חִבּוּר אָדָם הַנֶּאֱסָפִים יַחַד **חֶבֶר כֹּהֲנִים** הוּא **לִרְצֹחַ**. וְהָרִאשׁוֹן מִדִּבְרֵי רַבִּי מֵאִיר שְׁלִיחַ צִבּוּר זצ״ל:


**for they devised a plot** For this is a plot which they plotted prior to their gathering, to which place they would go to murder and to loot. וּכְחַכֵּי means ajjmedours in O.F, fishermen. And also in mishnaic Hebrew, חַכֵּי is an expression of joining. The Gemara states (Baba Kamma 119b): “And he shall not put into it more than three חַכִּין.” Those are the stitches with which the comber joins together both ends of the garment when he beats it with sticks. This [following] interpretation is mine, and it seems appropriate, and this is the explanation: And when there is a group of five men gathering together, it is a company of priests to murder. The first one is that of Rabbi Meir Sheliach Zibbur o.b.m.


###### Rashi on Hosea 6:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:9:3)


**שֶׁכְמָה.** לְשׁוֹן כַּת אַחַת:


**in one group** Heb. שֶׁכְמָה, an expression of one group.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Hosea 6:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:10:1)


**שַׁעֲרוּרִיָּה.** יוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם לְשׁוֹן ׳שִׁנּוּי׳, שֶׁשִּׁינּוּ אֶת דַּרְכָּם, וְכֵן: ״שַׁמָּה וְשַׁעֲרוּרִיָּה״ (ירמיה ה:ל):


**a horrible thing** Heb. שַׁעֲרוּרִיָה. Jonathan renders this as an expression of change, for they changed their way, and so: (Jer. 5:20) “an appalling and horrible thing (שֲעֲרוּרָה).”



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Hosea 6:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:11:1)


**גַּם יְהוּדָה.** אַף הוּא הוֹאִיל וְקִלְקֵל:


**Judah, too** Since they sinned.


###### Rashi on Hosea 6:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:11:2)


**שָׁת.** הַמֵּשִׁית זְמַן פֻּרְעָנוּת לָהּ:


**appointed** Heb. שָׁת. The appointer appointed a time for retribution for her.


###### Rashi on Hosea 6:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:11:3)


**קָצִיר.** עֵת סִלּוּקָהּ וְגָלוּתָהּ, כְּמוֹ ״עוֹד מְעַט וּבָאָה עֵת הַקָּצִיר לָהּ״ (ירמיה נא:לג), בְּפֻרְעָנוּת בָּבֶל בְּסֵפֶר יִרְמְיָה:


**harvest** The time of her leaving and her exile. Comp. “A little more time and the time of harvest shall come to her,” in reference to the retribution of Babylon, in the Book of Jeremiah (51:33).


###### Rashi on Hosea 6:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:11:4)


**בְּשׁוּבִי שְׁבוּת עַמִּי.** בְּהוֹכִיחַ אוֹתָם לְשׁוֹבֵב מְשׁוּבוֹתָם (ירמיה ג:כב):


**when I will return the backsliding of My people** When I admonish them to return from their backsliding.


###### Rashi on Hosea 6:11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/6:11:5)


**שְׁבוּת עַמִּי.** לְשׁוֹן ״מְשׁוּבָה״ (ירמיה ג:ו), נַעֲרוּת וְיוֹלְדוֹת. אינווייזור״א בְּלַעַ״ז. וְכֵן ״בָּנִים שׁוֹבָבִים״ (ירמיה ג:יד), ״הַבַּת הַשּׁוֹבֵבָה״ (ירמיה לא:כא):


**the backsliding of My people** Heb. שְׁבוּת, an expression of the straying of youth and childhood, anwejjsdoure in O.F. Comp. (Jer. 3:14) “backsliding children (שוֹבָבִים)”; (ibid. 31:21) “backsliding daughter (הַבַּת הַשׁוֹבֵבָה).”



## Chapter 7


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Hosea 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:1:1)


**כְּרָפְאִי לְיִשְׂרָאֵל.** כְּשֶׁאֲנִי חָפֵץ לְהוֹשִׁיעָם וְלִרְפּאוֹת עֲוֹנוֹתָם, נִגְלוּ לְפָנַי **כִּי פָּעֲלוּ** תָּמִיד **שֶׁקֶר וְגַנָּבִים** מֵהֶם תָּמִיד **בָּאִים** וְגוֹנְבִים מָמוֹן חֲבֵרֵיהֶם, וְאַף **פּוֹשְׁטִים גְּדוּדֵיהֶם** בְּלִסְטִיּוּת לְלַסְטֵם אֶת הַבְּרִיּוֹת:


**When I would heal Israel** When I wish to save them and heal them, their iniquities are revealed before Me, for they constantly commit falsehood, and thieves come constantly from them and steal their neighbors’ property, and also their bands spread out with lawlessness, to rob the people.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Hosea 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:2:1)


**וּבַל יֹאמְרוּ לִלְבָבָם.** אֵינָן נוֹתְנִין אֶל לִבָּם שֶׁכָּל רָעָתָם כְּתוּבָה לְזִכָּרוֹן לְפָנַי:


**They do not say in their heart** They do not lay it up to their heart that all their evil is inscribed before Me as a remembrance.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Hosea 7:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:3:1)


**יְשַׂמְּחוּ מֶלֶךְ.** מַלְכָּם חָפֵץ בָּרָעָה שֶׁהֵם עוֹשִׂים:


**they make the king glad** Their king desires the evil that they do.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Hosea 7:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:4:1)


**בּוֹעֵרָה מֵאֹפֶה.** שֶׁהֻסְּקָה מִיַּד הַנַּחְתּוֹם, כֵּן יִצְרָם בּוֹעֵר בְּקִרְבָּם:


**like an oven heated by the baker** that was heated by the baker, so does their lust burn within them.


###### Rashi on Hosea 7:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:4:2)


**יִשְׁבּוֹת מֵעִיר.** יוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם מַה שֶׁתִּרְגֵּם, וְאֵינִי יָכוֹל לְיַשֵּׁב בּוֹ לְשׁוֹן הַמִּקְרָא. וּלְפִי מַשְׁמָעוֹ, **יִשְׁבּוֹת** יִצְרָם הָרָע שֶׁהָיָה מֵעִירָם:


**he rests from awakening** Heb. יִשְׁבּוֹת מֵעִיר. Jonathan translated what he translated: I cannot reconcile it with the wording of the verse, and, according to its apparent meaning, their temptation, which arouses them, rests.


###### Rashi on Hosea 7:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:4:3)


**מִלּוּשׁ בָּצֵק עַד חֻמְצָתוֹ.** מִשֶּׁחָשַׁב בְּלִבּוֹ אֶת הָרָעָה כֵּיצַד יַעֲשֶׂה אוֹתָהּ, הוּא שׁוֹבֵת וְיָשֵׁן עַד הַבֹּקֶר שֶׁהוּא יָכוֹל לַעֲשׂוֹתָהּ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָאוֹפֶה שׁוֹבֵת מִלּוּשׁ בָּצֵק עַד שֶׁהֶחְמִיצָה שֶׁהוּא יָכוֹל לֶאֱפוֹת. וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר (מיכה ב:א): ״הוֹי חוֹשְׁבֵי אָוֶן וּפֹעֲלֵי רָע עַל מִשְׁכְּבוֹתָם בְּאוֹר הַבֹּקֶר יַעֲשׂוּהָ״:


**from the kneading the dough until it leavens** From the time he thinks of the evil in his heart, how he will do it, he rests and sleeps until morning, when he can do it, just as the baker rests from the kneading of the dough until it leavens, when he can bake it. And so it is stated in Micah (2: 1): “Woe is to those who plan iniquity and work evil on their beds; when the morning lights up, they do it.”



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Hosea 7:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:5:1)


**יוֹם מַלְכֵּנוּ.** בְּיוֹם שֶׁהָיָה מַמְלִיכִין אֶת מַלְכֵיהֶם:


**The day of our king** The day we crown our king.


###### Rashi on Hosea 7:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:5:2)


**הֶחֱלוּ שָׂרִים.** נַעֲשׂוּ שָׂרֵינוּ חוֹלִים:


**the princes became ill** Our princes became ill.


###### Rashi on Hosea 7:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:5:3)


**חֲמַת מִיָּיִן.** מֵחוֹם הַיַּיִן הַבּוֹעֵר בָּם, וְהַמֶּלֶךְ מוֹשֵׁךְ יָדוֹ מִן הַטּוֹבִים וְהַכְּשֵׁרִים לְהִתְחַבֵּר אֶת לוֹצְצִים (ירושלמי עבודה זרה א:א:ג):


**from the heat of the wine** From the heat of the wine which burns within them, and the king withdraws his hand from the good and proper people to join the scorners.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Hosea 7:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:6:1)


**כִּי קֵרְבוּ כַתַּנּוּר לִבָּם בְּאָרְבָּם.** הֵכִינוּ וְהִזְמִינוּ אֶת לִבָּם בְּמַעֲשֵׂה אוֹרֵב שֶׁלָּהֶם, כְּתַנּוּר זֶה שֶׁמְּכִינִים וּמַסִּיקִין אוֹתוֹ לֶאֱפוֹת:


**For they have made ready their heart like an oven with their ambush** They prepared and readied their heart with their ambush, like an oven which they prepare and heat in order to bake.


###### Rashi on Hosea 7:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:6:2)


**כָּל הַלַּיְלָה יָשֵׁן אוֹפֵיהֶם.** נַחְתּוֹם שֶׁלָּהֶם הַמַּסִּיק אֶת הַתַּנּוּר, לְאַחַר שֶׁהֵכִינוּ לִבָּם וְחָשְׁבוּ אֶת גְּמַר הָרָעָה אֵיךְ יַעֲשׂוּהָ:


**all night their baker sleeps** Their baker, who heats the oven after they have prepared their heart and have thought of the execution of the evil, how they would do it.


###### Rashi on Hosea 7:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:6:3)


**יָשֵׁן אוֹפֵיהֶם.** כְּלוֹמַר, עַד הַבֹּקֶר יְשֵׁנִים, וְלַבֹּקֶר בּוֹעֲרִים כָּאֵשׁ עַד שֶׁגָּמְרוּ אֶת רָעָתָם:


**their baker sleeps** i.e., until morning they sleep, and in the morning they burn like fire until they consummate their evil.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Hosea 7:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:7:1)


**וְאָכְלוּ אֶת שׁוֹפְטֵיהֶם.** הָרְגוּ אֶת סַנְהֶדְרִין שֶׁלָּהֶם, לְפִי שֶׁהָיוּ מוֹחִים בְּיָדָם. וּמָה עָלְתָה לָהֶם? כָּל מַלְכֵיהֶם נָפְלוּ, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין קוֹרֵא בָּהֶם אֵלַי. וּבִירוּשַׁלְמִי בְּמַסֶּכֶת עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה (א,א) דָּרְשׁוּ: בְּיוֹם שֶׁהִמְלִיכוּ אֶת יָרָבְעָם, הֵחֵלּוּ שָׂרִים חֲמַת מִיַּיִן (הושע ז:ה), כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ: אָתוֹן לְגַבֵּיהּ אָמְרִין לֵיהּ, ״קוּם עֲבֵד לָן עכום״. אָמַר לוֹן, ״הַשְׁתָּא עִתֵּי רַמְשָׁא הוּא וְכָל עַמָּא שָׁתְיִין״, כְּלוֹמַר עֵת שִׁכְרוּת הוּא, שֶׁמָּא עַכְשָׁיו שִׁכּוֹרִים אַתֶּם וּמָחָר תַּחְזְרוּ בָּכֶם. ״אִיזְלוּן וְאִיתוּ בְּצַפְרָא״. אָזְלוּ וְאָתוּ בְּצַפְרָא, הָדָא הִיא ״כִּי קֵרְבוּ כַתַּנּוּר וגו׳״ (הושע ז:ו). אָמַר לוֹן, ״אֲנָא דָחִיל מִן סַנְהֶדְרִין דִּילְכוֹן״. אָמְרוּן לֵיהּ, ״אֲנַן קָטְלִינַן לוֹן״. הָדָא הִיא **וְאָכְלוּ אֶת שׁוֹפְטֵיהֶם**.


**and they devour their judges** They slew their Sanhedrin because they protested against them, and what happened to them? **All their kings have fallen because** “none of them calls to me.” And in the Palestinian Talmud in tractate Avodah Zarah (1:1) it is expounded as follows: On the day they crowned Jeroboam, “the princes became ill from the heat of wine” as is explained. They came to him and said to him, “Get up! Make idols for us.” He replied, “Now is the time of evening, and all the people are drinking”; i.e., it is a time of drunkenness. Perhaps now you are drunk, and tomorrow you will recant. “Go and return in the morning.” They went and returned in the morning. That is what is written: “For they have made ready etc.” He said to them, “I fear your Sanhedrin.” They replied, “We will kill them.” I.e. what is written: “And they devour their judges.”


###### Rashi on Hosea 7:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:7:2)


**מָשַׁךְ יָדוֹ וְגוֹ׳.** כַּד הֲוָה חָמֵי בַּר נָשׁ כָּשֵׁר, הֲוָה מוֹתִיב גַּבֵּיהּ תְּרֵין לֵיצָנִין, וְאִינוּן אָמְרִין לוֹ: ״אֵיזֶה דּוֹר חָבִיב מִכָּל הַדּוֹרוֹת?״ וְהוּא אָמַר לוֹן: ״דּוֹר הַמִּדְבָּר״. וְאִינוּן אָמְרִין לוֹ: ״וְלֹא עָבְדִין עכום?״ וְהוּא אָמַר לוֹן: ״אַגַּב דְּהֲווֹ חֲבִיבִין לָא אִיתְעַנְשׁוּן״. וְאִינוּן אָמְרִין לוֹ: ״חַשׁ, דְּמַלְכָּא בָּעֵי לְמֶעְבַּד כֵּן״ – דּוֹם שֶׁהַמֶּלֶךְ רוֹצֶה לַעֲשׂוֹת כֵּן.


“He draws his hand etc.” When he saw a righteous man, he would seat two scorners next to him, and they would say to him, “Which generation was the most beloved of all generations?” And he would reply, “The generation of the desert.” And they would say to him, “Did they not worship idols?” And he would reply, “Since they were beloved, they were not punished.” And they would say to him, “Quiet! The king wants to do that.”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Hosea 7:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:8:1)


**יִתְבּוֹלָל.** יְבֻלְבַּל בַּגּוֹלָה בֵּין הָאֻמּוֹת:


**mingles** He will mingle in exile with the nations.


###### Rashi on Hosea 7:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:8:2)


**עֻגָה בְּלִי הֲפוּכָה.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״כְּחַרְרָה דְּעַד דְּלָא אִיתְהַפְּכַת אִיתְאַכְלַת״. עֻגָה חַרָרָה שֶׁאוֹפִין עַל הַגֶּחָלִים:


**as a cake which was not turned over** Jonathan renders: as a cake which, before being turned over, was consumed. עֻגָה is a cake which they bake on the coals.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Hosea 7:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:9:1)


**וְהוּא לֹא יָדַע.** לֹא שָׂם אֶל לִבּוֹ שֶׁאֲכָלוּהוּ מַלְכֵי אֲרָם בִּימֵי יְהוֹאָחָז, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (מלכים ב יג:ז): ״כִּי לֹא הִשְׁאִיר לִיהוֹאָחָז וגומר וַיְשִׂימֵם כֶּעָפָר לָדוּשׁ״, וְאַף עַל פִּי כֵן לֹא חָזַר יָרָבְעָם בְּנוֹ מִדַּעַת (מֵרָעַת ספרים אחרים) אָבִיו:


**but he did not know** He did not lay it up to his heart that the kings of Aram consumed him in the days of Jehoahaz, as it is said: (II Kings 13:7) “For he had not left to Jehoahaz etc. and made them like dust to trample.” Nonetheless, Jeroboam his son did not repent of his father’s evil.


###### Rashi on Hosea 7:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:9:2)


**גַּם שֵׂיבָה זָרְקָה** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״אַף חֲלִישׁוּתָא מְטַתִּינוֹן וְאִינוּן לָא מִסְתַּכְּלִין״:


**also weakness was cast** Heb. שֵׂיבָה, lit. ripe old age. Jonathan rendered. Also weakness reached them, but they did not contemplate it.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Hosea 7:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:10:1)


**וְעָנָה גְאוֹן יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּפָנָיו.** ״וּמְאַךְ יְקָרָא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל וְאִינוּן חֲזָן״, לְשׁוֹן ׳עֹנִי׳. וְאֵין וְעָנָה זֶה לְשׁוֹן עָתִיד אֶלָּא לְשׁוֹן עָבָר, כְּמוֹ ״וְקָרָא זֶה אֶל זֶה״ (ישעיה ו:ג) שֶׁמָּעְתִּי שֶׁקָּרָא זֶה אֶל זֶה, וּכְמוֹ ״וְעָשָׂה לוֹ כְּתֹנֶת פַּסִּים״ (בראשית לז:ג). וְאַף זֶה לְשׁוֹן עָבָר: כְּבָר נִמְעֲטוּ וְנִשְׁפְּלוּ, וּבְכָל זֹאת לֹא שָׁבוּ אֶל ה׳:


**And the pride of Israel was humbled before Him** Heb. וְעָנָה. And the glory of Israel was humbled and they saw; an expression of humility, and this is not a future tense but a past tense. Comp. (Isa. 6: 3) “And one called (וְקָרָא) to the other.” I heard that one called to the other. Also (Gen. 37:3) “and he made (וְעָשָׂה) for him a woolen shirt.” This too is a past tense. They have already been diminished and humbled, and, despite this, they did not return to the Lord.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Hosea 7:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:11:1)


**אֵין לֵב.** לְהַבְחִין מַה טוֹב לָהֶם וּמַה רַע לָהֶם:


**without a heart** to discern between what was good for them and what was bad for them.


###### Rashi on Hosea 7:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:11:2)


**מִצְרַיִם קָרָאוּ.** לְאוֹתָם שֶׁהֵרֵעוּ לָהֶם מֵעוֹלָם, הֵם קוֹרְאִים לְעֶזְרָה:


**they called upon Egypt** To those who have always harmed them, they call for aid.


###### Rashi on Hosea 7:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:11:3)


**אַשּׁוּר הָלָכוּ.** אֶל מֶלֶךְ אַשּׁוּר הָלָכוּ שֶׁיַּעֲזֹר לָהֶם:


**they went to Assyria** They went to the king of Assyria that he should aid them.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Hosea 7:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:12:1)


**כַּאֲשֶׁר יֵלְכוּ.** לְמִצְרָיִם:


**When they go** to Egypt.


###### Rashi on Hosea 7:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:12:2)


**אֶפְרֹשׂ עֲלֵיהֶם רִשְׁתִּי כְּעוֹף הַשָּׁמַיִם.** שֶׁהַנֶּשֶׁר וְהַנֵּץ עוֹלִין לְמַעְלָה מִמֶּנּוּ וּמוֹרִידִין אוֹתוֹ לָאָרֶץ, כֵּן אוֹרִידֵם בְּמִצְרַיִם עַל יְדֵי נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר:


**I will spread My net over them; like the fowl of the heaven** For the eagle and the hawk ascend higher than he and they bring them down to the earth, so will I bring them down in Egypt through Nebuchadnezzar. Another explanation: like the fowl of the heaven that is caught in a net hung on poles, and after they are in the net, they bring down the net.


###### Rashi on Hosea 7:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:12:3)


**אֲיַסְּרֵם כְּשֵׁמַע לַעֲדָתָם.** אָבִיא עֲלֵיהֶם יִסּוּרִים כְּכָל אֲשֶׁר אֲנִי מַשְׁמִיעַ לַעֲדָתָם בְּיַד יִרְמְיָה הַנָּבִיא, שֶׁעֲתִידִין הֵם לוֹמַר לוֹ: ״הִתְפַּלֵּל נָא בַּעֲדֵנוּ וְגוֹ׳״ (ירמיהו מב:ג), ״וְיַגֶּד לָנוּ ה׳ אֱלֹהֶיךָ וְגוֹמֵר אִם טוֹב וְאִם רָע וְגוֹ׳״ וְאָמַר לָהֶם: ״אִם שׂוֹם תָּשִׂימוּ נַפְשְׁכֶם לָשׁוּב מִצְרַיִם וְהָיְתָה הַחֶרֶב אֲשֶׁר אַתֶּם יְרֵאִים מִמֶּנָּה שָׁם תַּשִּׂיג אֶתְכֶם״ (ירמיהו מב:טו-טז), וְכָל הָעִנְיָן.


**I will chastise them as I let their congregation hear** I will bring chastisements upon them according to all that I let them hear through Jeremiah the prophet, that they are destined to say to him (42:2-6) “And pray in our behalf etc. And let the Lord your God tell etc. Whether good or bad etc.” And he said to them (verse 15f.), “If you direct your faces to come to Egypt etc. the sword that you fear will overtake you there,” and the entire section.


###### Rashi on Hosea 7:12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:12:4)


**אִיסִירֵם.** לְשׁוֹן ׳יִסּוּרִין׳. וּמְנַחֵם פֵּרֵשׁ: לְשׁוֹן ׳קֶשֶׁר׳ וּ״מוֹסְרוֹתֶיךָ אֲנַתֵּק״ (ירמיהו ל:ח). אֲבָל דּוֹנַשׁ פָּתַר: לְשׁוֹן ׳יִסּוּרִין׳, כְּמוֹ (ויקרא כו:כח) ״לְיַסֵּר אֶתְכֶם״. וְנוֹסַף הַיּוֹ״ד הַשֵּׁנִי בְּ״אִיסִירֵם״, כַּאֲשֶׁר נוֹסַף בְּ״יַיְשִׁירוּ״ – ״וְעַפְעַפֶּיךָ יַיְשִׁירוּ נֶגְדֶּךָ״ (משלי ד:כה). לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: **כְּעוֹף הַשָּׁמַיִם** הַנִּלְכָּד בָּרֶשֶׁת הַתְּלוּיָה עַל כְּלוֹנְסוֹת, וּלְאַחַר שֶׁהֵם בָּרֶשֶׁת, מוֹרִידִין הָרֶשֶׁת.


**I will chastise them** Heb. אַיְסִירֵם, an expression of chastisements (יִסּוּרִין) and Menahem (Machbereth Menahem pp. 128f.) explained [it as] an expression of binding. Comp. (Jer. 30:8) “and I will break your thongs (מוֹסְרוֹתֶיךָ),” but Dunash (Teshuvoth Dunash p. 34) interpreted [it as] an expression of chastisements (יִסּוּרִין). Comp. (Lev. 26:18) “to chastise you (לְיַסְרָה).” And the second “yud” in אַיְסִירֵם is added, as it is added in (יַיְשִׁירוּ) (Prov. 4:25) “and your eyelids look straight (יַיְשִירוּ) ahead of you.”



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Hosea 7:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:13:1)


**וַאֲנֹכִי אֶפְדֵּם.** אֲנִי הָיִיתִי חָפֵץ לִפְדוֹתָם מֵרָעָה אֲשֶׁר הֵם בָּהּ:


**I would redeem them** I wanted to redeem them from the evil in which they are [found].


###### Rashi on Hosea 7:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:13:2)


**וְהֵם דִּבְּרוּ עָלַי כְּזָבִים.** אָמְרוּ אֶל יִרְמְיָה: ״שֶׁקֶר אַתָּה מְדַבֵּר לֹא שְׁלָחֲךָ ה׳ לֵאמֹר אַל תָּבוֹאוּ מִצְרַיִם״ (ירמיה מג:ב):


**but they spoke lies about Me** They said to Jeremiah, (Jer. 43:2) “You speak falsely; the Lord our God did not send you to say, ‘You shall not come to Egypt.’”



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Hosea 7:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:14:1)


**כִּי יְיֵלִילוּ עַל מִשְׁכְּבוֹתָם.** כִּי עַל כֵּן תָּבֹא לָהֶם יְלָלָה, כָּךְ תַּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן:


**therefore, they will wail on their beds** Because, therefore, a wail will come to them. So did Jonathan render it.


###### Rashi on Hosea 7:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:14:2)


**יִתְגּוֹרָרוּ.** יֵשׁ לָהֶם מְגוּרוֹת שֶׁל דָּגָן, לְפִיכָךְ יָסוּרוּ וְיִמְרְדוּ בִּי:


**they store up** They have many granaries of corn; therefore, they turn away and rebel against Me.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Hosea 7:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:15:1)


**וַאֲנִי יִסַּרְתִּי חִזַּקְתִּי זְרוֹעֹתָם.** בְּיִרְאָתִי, עַל יְדֵי נְבִיאַי, לִשְׁמֹעַ אֲלֵיהֶם בְּמַה שֶׁהֵם מְיַסְּרִים אוֹתָם, וְיִתְחַזְּקוּ זְרוֹעוֹתָם:


**But I chastised them, I strengthened their arms** with My fear, through My prophets, to hearken to them with what they were chastising them, and their arms would be strengthened.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Hosea 7:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:16:1)


**יָשׁוּבוּ לֹא עָל** שָׁבוּ לְמִצְרַיִם וְלֹא לְהוֹעִיל לָהֶם, לְכָךְ נָקוּד עָל בְּקָמַץ, כְּמוֹ (שמואל ב כג:א) ״נְאֻם הַגֶּבֶר הוּקַם עָל״, שֶׁהוּא לְשׁוֹן ׳עִלּוּי׳ וְאֵינוֹ דָבוּק לַתֵּבָה שֶׁלְּאַחֲרָיו:


**They will return, but to no avail** Heb. עָל. They returned to Egypt, but it did not avail them. Therefore, עָל is vowelized with a “kamatz.” Comp. (II Sam. 23:1) “The word of the man raised up high (עָל),” which is an expression of raising up and is not attached to the following word.


###### Rashi on Hosea 7:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:16:2)


**הָיוּ כְּקֶשֶׁת רְמִיָּה.** כְּשֶׁרוֹצֶה לִירוֹת בּוֹ חֵץ לַצָּפוֹן הוּא יוֹרֶה לַדָּרוֹם:


**they were like a deceitful bow** which, when one wishes to shoot with it toward the north, shoots toward the south.


###### Rashi on Hosea 7:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:16:3)


**יִפְּלוּ בַחֶרֶב שָׂרֵיהֶם.** יוֹחָנָן בֶּן קָרֵחַ (ירמיהו מג:ד) וְכָל הַחַיָּלִים שֶׁשָּׁבוּ לְמִצְרַיִם:


**their princes shall fall by the sword** Jonathan son of Kareah and all the officers of the armies who returned to Egypt.


###### Rashi on Hosea 7:16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:16:4)


**מִזַּעַם לְשׁוֹנָם.** לָשׁוֹן קָשָׁה שֶׁהֵשִׁיבוּ לְיִרְמְיָה (ירמיה מד:טז):


**from the fury of their tongue** The harsh language with which they replied to Jeremiah.


###### Rashi on Hosea 7:16:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:16:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/7:16:5)


**זוֹ לַעְגָּם.** אֲשֶׁר יַלְעִיגוּ לָהֶם בְּאֶרֶץ מִצְרַיִם, לָמָּה שַׁבְתֶּם אֶצְלֵנוּ כְּדֵי לְהָבִיא עָלֵינוּ פֻּרְעָנִיּוֹת, וַהֲלֹא כָּתוּב לָכֶם ״לֹא תוֹסִיפוּ לִרְאוֹתָם עוֹד״ (שמות יד:יג):


**This is their derision** which they will deride them in the land of Egypt. Why have you returned to us to bring retribution upon us? Is it not written concerning you,” (Ex. 14:13) “You shall no longer continue to see them”?



## Chapter 8


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Hosea 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:1:1)


**אֶל חִכְּךָ שׁוֹפָר.** שְׁכִינָה אוֹמֶרֶת לַנָּבִיא: הַשְׁמַע קוֹל חִכְּךָ וּקְרָא כְּשׁוֹפָר, וְאוֹמֵר: עוּפוּ וּמַהֲרוּ הָאוֹיְבִים כַּאֲשֶׁר יִדְאֶה **הַנֶּשֶׁר** (דברים כח:מט) וּבָא **אֶל בֵּית ה׳. אֶל חִכְּךָ שׁוֹפָר**, כְּאִלּוּ הַשּׁוֹפָר נָתוּן אֶל חִכְּךָ:


**To your palate a shophar** The Shechinah says to the prophet: Let the voice of your palate be heard, and call like a shophar, and say: You enemies! Fly and hasten as the eagle swoops and come to the house of the Lord. To your palate a shophar, it is as though a shophar is placed to your palate. [after Jonathan]



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Hosea 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:2:1)


**לִי יִזְעָקוּ.** כְּשֶׁתָּבוֹא עֲלֵיהֶם הָרָעָה:


**To Me...will cry** when evil befalls them...


###### Rashi on Hosea 8:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:2:2)


**אֱלֹהַי יְדַעֲנוּךָ.** הֲלֹא עַמְּךָ אֲנַחְנוּ וְסָרְסוּהוּ וּדְרָשׁוּהוּ: **לִי יִזְעָקוּ** יִשְׂרָאֵל **אֱלֹהַי יְדַעֲנוּךָ**, וַאֲנִי לֹא אֶשְׁמַע לָהֶם, כִּי


**“My God! We know You”** Are we not Your people? Transpose it and interpret it. Israel will cry to Me, “My God! We know You!” But I will not heed them, for...



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Hosea 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:3:1)


**זָנַח יִשְׂרָאֵל טוֹב.** לְפִיכָךְ **אוֹיֵב יִרְדְּפוֹ**:


**Israel has cast off the Good One** Therefore, the enemy shall pursue him.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Hosea 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:4:1)


**הֵשִׂירוּ.** עָשׂוּ שָׂרִים. דָּבָר אַחֵר: הֵסִירוּ אֶת זֶה מִמְּלוּכָה וְהִמְלִיכוּ אֶת זֶה, וּבְסֵפֶר הַמָּסֹרֶת מוֹכִיחַ כֵּן, שֶׁחִבְּרוֹ בַּמָּסוֹרָה כָּתְבִין שִׁי״ן וְקָרִיָּן סָמֶ״ךְ, וּפִתְרוֹנוֹ סָמֶ״ךְ:


**they removed** Heb. הֵשִׁירוּ, they set up princes (שָּׂרִים). Another explanation: [הֵשִּׂירוּ is like הֵסִירוּ, they removed,] they removed this one from the throne and crowned this one, and in the book of the Masorah, this is proven, for he classified it in the Masorah of those written with a “shin” and read as a “samech,” bearing the meaning of the “samech.”


###### Rashi on Hosea 8:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:4:2)


**לְמַעַן יִכָּרֵת.** לְמַעַן כִּי הָיָה לָהֶם זָהָב לָרֹב וְגָרְמוּ לָהֶם שֶׁ**יִכָּרֵת** הָעֹשֶׁר מֵהֶם:


**in order that it be cut off** For they had much gold, and they caused for them that the wealth be cut off from them.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Hosea 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:5:1)


**זָנַח.** הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא אֶתְכֶם בְּנֵי שׁוֹמְרוֹן בַּעֲוֹן עֲגָלִים שֶׁבְּבֵית אֵל (מלכים א יב:כח-כט), שֶׁמַּלְכֵי שׁוֹמְרוֹן עוֹבְדִים שָׁם:


**He has forsaken** The Holy One, blessed be He, has forsaken you, children of Samaria, for the iniquity of the calves in Bethel, where the kings of Israel worship.


###### Rashi on Hosea 8:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:5:2)


**עַד מָתַי לֹא יוּכְלוּ** לְהַפְנוֹת לִבָּם לְהִנָּקוֹת מֵאוֹתוֹ טִנּוּף:


**how long will they be unable** to empty their hearts to be cleansed of that filth.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Hosea 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:6:1)


**כִּי מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל וְהוּא.** מִכַּסְפָּם וּזְהָבָם שֶׁל כָּל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד הוּא גָּבוּי וְהֶחָרָשׁ עָשָׂהוּ. וְהַוָּי״ו שֶׁל ׳וְהוּא׳ נוֹפֵל, וְיֵשׁ הַרְבֵּה וָי״ו דּוֹמִין לוֹ (יחזקאל מו): ״בְּצֵאתוֹ וְגֵבָאיו וְלֹא יֵרָפְאוּ״, אַף כָּאן כִּי מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל הַזְמָנָתוֹ וַהֲכָנָתוֹ וְהוּא נַעֲשָׂה. וְיֵשׁ לְיַשֵּׁב הַוָּי״ו הַזֹּאת כֵּן כִּי מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל וְהוּא אַף הָעֵגֶל הַזֶּה מֵאִתָּם בָּא כְּמוֹת הָעֵגֶל הָרִאשׁוֹן:


**For it is from Israel** From each one’s silver and gold it was collected, and the craftsman made it. And the “vav” of וְהוּא is superfluous, and there are many like it, e.g. (Ezekiel 47:11), “Its swamps and its pools shall not become healthy (וְלֹא יֵרָפְאוּ).” Here too, for its preparation is from Israel and it is made. And this “vav” may be reconciled in the following manner: כִּי מִיִשְׂרָאֵל וְהוּא Also, this calf came from them like the first calf.


###### Rashi on Hosea 8:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:6:2)


**שְׁבָבִים.** ״לִנְסָרֵי לְוָחִין״, וְלָשׁוֹן אֲרַמִּית הוּא שִׁיבָא מִכְּשׁוּרָא (סנהדרין ז,ב):


**splinters** Heb. שְׁבָבִים, Jonathan renders: splinters of boards, and it is an Aramaic expression. cf. שִּׁיבָא מִכְּשּׁוּרָא (Sanhedrin 7b), a splinter of a beam.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Hosea 8:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:7:1)


**קָמָה אֵין לוֹ.** לַזֶּרַע שֶׁלָּהֶם, כְּלוֹמַר לֹא יַצְלִיחוּ בְּמַעֲשֵׂיהֶם:


**it has no standing grain** Their seed has no standing grain; i.e., they will not prosper with their deeds.


###### Rashi on Hosea 8:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:7:2)


**אוּלַי יַעֲשֶׂה.** אַף אִם יַעֲשֶׂה קֶמַח:


**if it does produce** Even if it produces flour.


###### Rashi on Hosea 8:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:7:3)


**זָרִים יִבְלָעוּהוּ.** אַף אִם יַצְלִיחוּ בְּנִכְסֵיהֶם, סוֹפוֹ לִפּוֹל בְּיַד זָרִים:


**strangers will swallow it** Even if they prosper with their property, it will eventually fall into the hands of strangers.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Hosea 8:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:9:1)


**פֶּרֶא בּוֹדֵד לוֹ.** נַעֲשׂוּ כְּפֶרֶא שֶׁהוֹלֵךְ בּוֹדֵד לְעַצְמוֹ, שׁוֹאֵף רוּחַ (ירמיה ב:כד) מִמָּקוֹם לְמָקוֹם לָנוּד:


**a wild donkey secluded to himself** He became like a wild donkey that goes alone to itself, sniffing air from place to place to wander.


###### Rashi on Hosea 8:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:9:2)


**הִתְנוּ אֲהָבִים.** יוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם לְשׁוֹן ׳תַּנִּים׳, כְּמוֹ עָרוֹד זֶה שֶׁהוֹלֵךְ בַּמִּדְבָּרוֹת. ״שָׁאֲפוּ רוּחַ כַּתַּנִּים״ (ירמיה יד:ו) לִקְנוֹת לָהֶם אוֹהֲבִים נוֹאֲפִים זָרִים. **הִתְנוּ** – אידגרוניו״ט בְּלַעַ״ז, וְיֵשׁ לְפָרֵשׁ לְשׁוֹן ״אֶתְנָן״ (הושע ט:א):


**they paid hire for love** Heb. הִתְנוּ. Jonathan interprets it as an expression of a dragon; like this dragon that goes through the deserts. They sniffed the air like the dragon to acquire for themselves lovers, strange paramours. הִתְנוּ, endragonיrיnt in O.F., like the tremors of the nostrils of a dragon, [derived from תַּנִין. According to this derivation we render: dragonlike, they sought lovers.] It may also be explained as an expression of hire.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Hosea 8:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:10:1)


**גַּם כִּי יִתְּנוּ בַגּוֹיִם.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁעָשׂוּ זֹאת, שֶׁהִתְנוּ אֲהָבִים בְּעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת:


**Although they pay hire among the nations** Although they did this, that they paid for lovers of the nations.


###### Rashi on Hosea 8:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:10:2)


**עַתָּה אֲקַבְּצֵם** בְּבֹא עֵת גְּאֻלָּתָם לֹא אֲעַכְּבֶנָּה:


**now I will gather them** When the time of their redemption arrives, I will not delay it.


###### Rashi on Hosea 8:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:10:3)


**וַיָּחֵלּוּ מְעַט מִמַּשָּׂא מֶלֶךְ שָׂרִים.** וְיִכָּנְעוּ מְעַט בְּגָלוּת בְּבָבֶל מִיִּרְאַת מַשָּׂא מֶלֶךְ שָׂרִים, מֵעֹל הָאֻמּוֹת שֶׁהָיוּ בְּתוֹכָם:


**and they will be humbled a little from the burden of the king and the princes** And they will be humbled a little in their exile from the fear of the burden of the king and the princes, i.e. from the yoke of the kings of the nations among whom they were.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Hosea 8:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:11:1)


**כִּי הִרְבָּה אֶפְרַיִם** לְפִיכָךְ אֲחִילֶנּוּ **מִמַּשָּׂא מֶלֶךְ שָׂרִים**:


**For Ephraim has increased** Therefore, I will humble them with the burden of the king and the princes.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Hosea 8:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:12:1)


**אֶכְתֹּב לוֹ.** אֲנִי תָמִיד מוֹכִיחוֹ עַל יְדֵי נְבִיאַי, וְכוֹתֵב לוֹ רֻבֵּי תוֹרָתִי וְהֵם כְּמוֹ זָר נֶחְשָׁבוּ:


**I write for them** I constantly reprimand them through My prophets, and write for them the great things of My Law, but they are considered a strange thing.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Hosea 8:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:13:1)


**זִבְחֵי הַבְהָבַי.** זִבְחֵי עוֹלוֹתַי שֶׁהֵם מְהַבְהֲבִים לְפָנַי בָּאֵשׁ שֶׁעַל מִזְבְּחִי:


**As for the sacrifices they burn before Me** Heb. זִבְחֵי הַבְהָבַי. The sacrifice of My burnt offerings that they scorch in the fire which is upon My altar.


###### Rashi on Hosea 8:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/8:13:2)


**יִזְבְּחוּ בָשָׂר וַיֹּאכֵלוּ.** לָמָּה לִי שֶׁמְּבִיאִים אוֹתָם? יִזְבְּחוּ לִהְיוֹת לָהֶם בָּשָׂר לְמַאֲכָלָם וַיֹּאכְלוּם, כִּי אֵינֶנִּי רוֹצֶה אוֹתָם (עמוס ה:כא-כב):


**let them slaughter the flesh and eat [it]** Why do I need them to bring them? Let them slaughter so that they have flesh to eat and eat them, for I do not desire them.



## Chapter 9


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Hosea 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:1:1)


**אַל תִּשְׂמַח יִשְׂרָאֵל** כְּשָׁאָר עוֹבְדֵי עֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים הַקַּדְמוֹנִים, שֶׁהֵם לֹא קִבְּלוּ תּוֹרָה וְלֹא בָּאוּ לְחֶלְקִי, וְאַתָּה זָנִיתָ מֵעָלַי וְאִבַּדְתָּ טוֹבוֹת הַרְבֵּה:


**Rejoice not, O Israel** like the other peoples, for they did not receive a Torah, and they did not come to My lot, but you strayed from Me and lost many benefits.


###### Rashi on Hosea 9:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:1:2)


**אָהַבְתָּ.** לִהְיוֹת כְּזוֹנָה לְקַבֵּל **אֶתְנָן**:


**you love** to be like a harlot, to receive a harlot’s hire.


###### Rashi on Hosea 9:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:1:3)


**עַל כָּל גְּרָנוֹת.** כְּדֶרֶךְ זוֹנָה שֶׁנּוֹתְנִין לָהּ דָּגָן בַּגֹּרֶן בְּאֶתְנַנָּהּ (בראשית רבה נז:ד):


**on all threshing floors of corn** In the manner of the harlot to whom they give corn on the threshing floor for her hire. [after Gen. Rabbah 57:4]



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Hosea 9:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:3:1)


**לֹא יֵשְׁבוּ בְּאֶרֶץ ה׳.** לֹא יִתְעַכְּבוּ בְּאַרְצָם:


**They shall not stay in the land of the Lord** Heb. יֵשְׁבוּ, they shall not stay in their land.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Hosea 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:4:1)


**לֹא יִסְּכוּ.** לְשׁוֹן ׳נֶסֶךְ׳:


**They shall not pour libations** Heb. יִסְּכוּ, an expression of נֶסֶךְ, libation.


###### Rashi on Hosea 9:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:4:2)


**לֹא יֶעֶרְבוּ לוֹ.** לֹא תְּהֵא מִנְחָתָם עֲרֵבָה עָלָיו:


**they will not please Him** Their offering will not be pleasant to Him.


###### Rashi on Hosea 9:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:4:3)


**כְּלֶחֶם אוֹנִים.** כְּלֶחֶם גְּזֵלָה הַבָּא עַל יְדֵי כֹּחַ וְאוֹנִים (ישעיה מ:כו):


**like bread of mourners** Heb. אוֹנִים, like bread of robbery that comes through strength and might.


###### Rashi on Hosea 9:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:4:4)


**כִּי לַחְמָם לְנַפְשָׁם** רָאוּי הוּא זֶה לָהֶם לְמַאֲכָל וְלֹא לָבוֹא לְבֵית ה׳. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **כְּלֶחֶם אוֹנִים** כְּלֶחֶם אֲבֵלִים שֶׁהוּא טָמֵא (דברים כו:יד), וְסוֹפוֹ מוֹכִיחַ **כָּל אוֹכְלָיו יִטְמָאוּ**:


**for their bread is for themselves** It is fit for them for food, but not to come into the house of the Lord. Another explanation is: אוֹנִים, like bread of mourners, which is contaminated, and the end of the verse proves it: all those who partake thereof become contaminated.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Hosea 9:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:5:1)


**לְיוֹם מוֹעֵד.** לְיוֹם זְמַן קָבוּעַ לִהְיוֹת הָאוֹיֵב נוֹעָד לָבֹא עֲלֵיכֶם:


**for the appointed day** For the day of the set time for the enemy to be appointed to come upon you.


###### Rashi on Hosea 9:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:5:2)


**חַג ה׳.** זֶבַח הֲרִיגָה שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ בָּכֶם:


**the Lord’s massacre** Heb. חַג, a slaughter, the killing which I have in you.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Hosea 9:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:6:1)


**כִּי הִנֵּה הָלְכוּ** מִמְּקוֹמָם מֵרֹב שׁוֹדְדִים שֶׁיָּבֹאוּ עֲלֵיהֶם עִם נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר:


**For behold, they have gone away** from their place because of the many plunderers who will come upon them with Nebuchadnezzar.


###### Rashi on Hosea 9:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:6:2)


**מוֹף.** מֵאֶרֶץ מִצְרַיִם הִיא:


**Moph shall bury them** It is of the land of Egypt.


###### Rashi on Hosea 9:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:6:3)


**מַחְמַד לְכַסְפָּם.** בָּתֵּי חֶמְדַּת אוֹצְרוֹת כַּסְפָּם:


**the treasurehouses for their silver** The houses of the precious treasures of their silver.


###### Rashi on Hosea 9:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:6:4)


**קִימּוֹשׁ. חוֹחַ** מִינֵי חַיּוֹת תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן:


**thistles etc. briers** Heb. קִימוֹשׁ חוֹחַ. Jonathan renders these terms as species of beasts.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Hosea 9:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:7:1)


**הַפְּקֻדָּה.** שֶׁיִּפָּקְדוּ עֲוֹנוֹתֵיכֶם:


**the visitation** When your sins will be visited.


###### Rashi on Hosea 9:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:7:2)


**הַשִּׁלּוּם.** ״תּוֹשְׁלָמַת חוֹבִין״:


**recompense** the payment of sins.


###### Rashi on Hosea 9:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:7:3)


**אֱוִיל הַנָּבִיא.** אַף מִנְּבִיאֵי אֱמֶת שֶׁלָּהֶם יִהְיוּ אֱוִילִים, כְּגוֹן חֲנַנְיָה בֶּן עַזּוּר (ירמיה כח:א) שֶׁתְּחִלָּתוֹ נְבִיא אֱמֶת הָיָה:


**the prophet shall be a fool** Even some of their true prophets will be fools, e.g. Hananiah son of Azzur, who was originally a true prophet.


###### Rashi on Hosea 9:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:7:4)


**וְרַבָּה מַשְׂטֵמָה.** שֶׁהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא שׂוֹנֵא מַעֲשֵׂיכֶם.


**and the hatred is great** which the Holy One, blessed be He, hates your deeds.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Hosea 9:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:8:1)


**צוֹפֶה אֶפְרַיִם עִם אֱלֹהָי.** הֵם מַעֲמִידִין לָהֶם נְבִיאֵיהֶם, הַמַּמְשִׁיכִין אוֹתָן לְצַד עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה שֶׁלָּהֶם:


**Ephraim is a prophet with my God** They set up for themselves their prophets, who attract them toward their idolatry.


###### Rashi on Hosea 9:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:8:2)


**נָבִיא פַּח יָקוּשׁ עַל כָּל דְּרָכָיו.** וְלִנְבִיאֵי הָאֱמֶת הֵם טוֹמְנִין פַּחִים לְלָכְדָם:


**[for the] prophet [they lay] a fowler’s snare on all his ways** And for the true prophet they conceal snares to trap them.


###### Rashi on Hosea 9:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:8:3)


**מַשְׂטֵמָה בְּבֵית אֱלֹהָיו.** בְּבֵית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ הָרְגוּ אֶת זְכַרְיָה (דברי הימים ב כד:כא), וַיְבַקְּשׁוּ לַהֲרֹג אֶת יִרְמְיָה, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (ירמיהו כו:יא): ״מִשְׁפַּט מָוֶת לָאִישׁ הַזֶּה וְגוֹ׳״:


**[causing] hatred in the house of his God** In the Temple they assassinated Zechariah (II Chron. 24:21), and they sought to assassinate Jeremiah, according to the matter that is stated: (Jer. 26: 11) “This man is liable to death etc.”



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Hosea 9:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:9:1)


**כִּימֵי הַגִּבְעָה.** יֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים זֶה גִּבְעַת בִּנְיָמִין בְּפִלֶּגֶשׁ (שופטים יט), וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים זֶה גִּבְעַת שָׁאוּל (שמואל א יא:ד), שֶׁשָּׁאֲלוּ לָהֶם מֶלֶךְ וּמָרְדוּ בְּדִבְרֵי הַנָּבִיא:


**as in the days of Gibeah** Some say that this is Gibeah of Benjamin in the case of the concubine (Jud. 19), and some say that this is Gibeah of Saul, where they asked for a king and rebelled against the words of the prophet (I Sam. 15).



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Hosea 9:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:10:1)


**כַּעֲנָבִים בַּמִּדְבָּר.** כַּעֲנָבִים שֶׁהֵם חֲבִיבִים בַּמִּדְבָּר, כָּךְ חִבַּבְתִּי אֶת **יִשְׂרָאֵל**:


**Like grapes in the desert** Like grapes, which are precious in the desert, so did I love Israel.


###### Rashi on Hosea 9:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:10:2)


**כְּבִכּוּרָה בִתְאֵנָה.** כִּתְאֵנָה מְבֻשֶּׁלֶת בְּעֵץ הַתְּאֵנָה:


**like a ripe fig** Heb. כְּבִכּוּרָה, like a ripe fig. **on a fig tree** Heb. בִתְאֵנָהּ.


###### Rashi on Hosea 9:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:10:3)


**בְּרֵאשִׁיתָהּ.** בִּתְחִלַּת בִּשּׁוּל הַתְּאֵנִים:


**in its beginning** In the beginning of the ripening of the figs.


###### Rashi on Hosea 9:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:10:4)


**רָאִיתִי אֲבוֹתֵיכֶם.** כֵּן נִרְאוּ אֲבוֹתֵיכֶם בְּעֵינַי לְחַבְּבָם:


**I saw your forefathers** So did your forefathers appear in My eyes to love them.


###### Rashi on Hosea 9:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:10:5)


**וַיִּנָּזְרוּ.** אֵין ׳נְזִירָה׳ בְּכָל מָקוֹם אֶלָּא לְשׁוֹן ׳פְּרִישָׁה׳:


**and they separated themselves** Heb. וַיִנָּזְרוּ. Nowhere is נְזִירָה anything but an expression of separation.


###### Rashi on Hosea 9:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:10:6)


**לַבֹּשֶׁת.** לַעֲבוֹדָה זָרָה שֶׁהִיא בָּשְׁתָּם:


**to a shameful thing** To the deity, Baal-peor, which is their shame.


###### Rashi on Hosea 9:10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:10:7)


**וַיִּהְיוּ שִׁקּוּצִים** לְפָנַי:


**and they became detestable** before Me.


###### Rashi on Hosea 9:10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:10:8)


**כְּאָהֳבָם.** אֶת בְּנוֹת מוֹאָב:


**when they loved** the daughters of Moab.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Hosea 9:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:11:1)


**אֶפְרַיִם כָּעוֹף יִתְעוֹפֵף וְגוֹ׳.** אָמַר הַנָּבִיא הַלְוַאי וְיִהְיוּ כָּעוֹף הַזֶּה הַנּוֹדֵד מִקִּנּוֹ וְחָדֵל מִפְּרִיָּה וּרְבִיָּה, כָּךְ יִשְׁכְּלוּ אֶת זַרְעָם אוֹ בִּשְׁעַת **לֵדָה**, אוֹ יִתְעַכֵּל **בַּבֶּטֶן** וְיַפִּילוּ, אוֹ בִּשְׁעַת **הֵרָיוֹן** לֹא יִקְלְטוּ, כִּי מַה בֶּצַע בְּגַדְּלָם אֶת בְּנֵיהֶם שֶׁהֵם יְגַדְּלוּ וְשִׁכַּלְתִּים מֵאָדָם (הושע ט:יב):


**As for Ephraim their honor shall fly away like a bird etc.** Said the prophet: If only they would be like a bird that wanders from its nest and ceases to multiply, so should they be bereft of their seed, either at the time of birth, or that it should disintegrate and they should miscarry, or at the time of conception that they should not conceive, for what use is it that they raise their children, since, if they raise them, I will bereave them before they become men.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Hosea 9:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:12:1)


**כִּי גַם.** לָאָבוֹת.


**for woe... also** Woe to the fathers.


###### Rashi on Hosea 9:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:12:2)


**אוֹי לָהֶם בְּשׂוּרִי מֵהֶם.** כְּמוֹ בְּסוּרִי מֵהֶן, וְהוּא מִן מָסוֹרוֹת כָּתְבִין שִׁי״ן וְקָרִיין סָמֶ״ךְ. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **כְּעוֹף יִתְעוֹפֵף כְּבוֹדָם וְגוֹ׳,** כְּעוֹף זֶה שֶׁפּוֹרֵחַ וְאֵינֶנּוּ, כֵּן יָעוּף וְיִבְרַח כָּל כְּבוֹדָם. כָּל מַה שֶׁנִּצְטַעֲרוּ בִּבְנֵיהֶם בְּלֵידָה וּבְהֵרָיוֹן: מִלֵּידָה – זֶה צַעַר לֵידָה, וּמִבֶּטֶן – אֵלּוּ יְמֵי הָעִבּוּר, וּמֵהֵרָיוֹן – בִּשְׁעַת תַּשְׁמִישׁ. כָּל טָרְחָם זֶה יִכְלֶה. כֵּיצַד? שֶׁהֲרֵי כְּשֶׁיַגְדְּלוּ אֶת בְּנֵיהֶם וְשִׁכַּלְתִּים:


**when I turn away from them** Heb. בְּשוּרִי, like בְּסוּרִי, when I turn away from them. This is one of the words listed in the Masorah, which are spelled with a “sin” but read with a “sammech.” Another explanation: **their honor shall fly away like a bird etc.** Like this bird which flies away and is no more, so will their honor fly away and flee, all that they toiled with their children in birth, [in the womb,] and in conception. **From birth** This refers to the birth pangs. **and from the womb** This refers to the days of pregnancy. **and from conception** At the time of coitus. All this toil of theirs will end. How so? For, when they raise their children, I will bereave them.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Hosea 9:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:13:1)


**אֶפְרַיִם כַּאֲשֶׁר רָאִיתִי לְצוֹר** בְּשַׁלְוָתָהּ מַעֲטִירָה (ישעיה כג:ח) וַעֲשִׁירָה עַל כָּל הַמְּדִינוֹת, כֵּן רָאִיתִי לְאֶפְרַיִם שְׁתוּלָה בְּנָוֶה. וְאֶפְרַיִם מַה גְּמוּל שִׁלֵּם לִי? נִתְעַסֵּק לְהוֹצִיא אֶל הוֹרֵג בָּנָיו לְזָבְחָם לַעֲבוֹדָה זָרָה שֶׁל מֹלֶךְ. כֵּן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן:


**Ephraim, as I saw Tyre** in its tranquility, enjoying royalty and wealth more than all the provinces, so did I see Ephraim, planted in a dwelling place. But Ephraim what recompense did he recompense Me? He occupied himself with taking his children out to the slayer, to slaughter them in worship of the idols. So did Jonathan translate.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Hosea 9:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:14:1)


**תֵּן לָהֶם ה׳ מַה תִּתֵּן.** הַנָּבִיא מִתְפַּלֵּל שֶׁיָּמוּתוּ קְטַנִּים, שֶׁאֵינוֹ דוֹמֶה אֶבְלוֹ שֶׁל קָטָן לְאֶבְלוֹ שֶׁל גָּדוֹל (פסיקתא רבתי מד). לְפִיכָךְ אִם אִי אֶפְשָׁר מִלֵּדָה וּמִבֶּטֶן וּמֵהֵרָיוֹן, שֶׁכְּבָר נִגְזַר מִבְּרִיאַת עוֹלָם (בראשית ג:טז) ״וְאֶל אִישֵׁךְ תְּשׁוּקָתֵךְ״, תֵּן לָהֶם מְהֵרָה בְּקַטְנוּתָם מַה שֶׁאַתָּה אוֹמֵר לִתֵּן לָהֶם לְאַחַר זְמַן, שֶׁאָמַרְתָּ ״כִּי אִם יְגַדְּלוּ אֶת בְּנֵיהֶם וְשִׁכַּלְתִּים״:


**Give them, O Lord, what You will give** The prophet prays that they should die when young, for the mourning for a child is not as bad as the mourning for an adult. Therefore, if it is impossible that they die from birth, from the womb, or from conception, for it was already decreed from the Creation of the world, (Gen. 3: l6) “And to your husband shall be your longing,” give them quickly in their childhood what you say to give to them after a time, for You said, “For, if they raise their children. I will bereave them.” [from Pesikta Rabbathi 45:3]


###### Rashi on Hosea 9:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:14:2)


**תֵּן לָהֶם רֶחֶם מַשְׁכִּיל.** בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁיּוֹצְאִין לַאֲוִיר עוֹלָם יָמוּתוּ, אוֹ יִצְטַמְּקוּ שְׁדֵי אִמָּן שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא לָהֶם חָלָב לְהָנִיק:


**give them a bereaving womb** When they emerge into the world (lit. the air of the world), let them die or let their mother’s breasts dry up so that they will have no milk to nurse them.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Hosea 9:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:15:1)


**כָּל רָעָתָם בַּגִּלְגָּל.** שָׁם הִרְבּוּ לַעֲבֹד עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה בַּבָּמוֹת. לְפִי שֶׁהָיָה שָׁם הַמִּשְׁכָּן בַּתְּחִלָּה (יהושע ד:יט), הָיוּ אוֹמְרִים לָהֶם נְבִיאֵי הַבַּעַל שֶׁהוּא מָקוֹם נִבְחָר, וְהוּא הָיָה לְמַלְכֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**All their evil is in Gilgal** There they worshipped idols to a great extent (on high places absent in mss.). Since the mishkan was there first, the prophets of Baal would tell them that was a choice site, and it belonged to the kings of Israel.


###### Rashi on Hosea 9:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:15:2)


**לֹא אוֹסִף אַהֲבָתָם.** לֶאֱהוֹב אוֹתָם:


**I will not continue to love them** Heb. לֹא אוֹסֵף אַהֲבָתָם, lit. I will not continue their love.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Hosea 9:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:16:1)


**פְּרִי בַל יַעֲשׂוּן.** לֹא טוֹב לָהֶם לִפְרוֹת וְלִרְבּוֹת, שֶׁהֲרֵי **גַּם כִּי יֵלְדוּ וְהֵמַתִּי מַחֲמַדֵּי בִטְנָם**:


**they shall not produce fruit** It is not good for them to multiply, for, even if they bear children, I will slay the precious ones of their womb.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Hosea 9:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/9:17:1)


**יִמְאָסֵם אֱלֹהַי.** אֵינֶנּוּ קְלָלָה אֶלָּא בְּשׂוֹרָה, עָתִיד הוּא לְמָאֳסָם:


**My God shall reject them** This is not a curse but a report. He is destined to reject them.



## Chapter 10


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Hosea 10:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:1:1)


**גֶּפֶן בּוֹקֵק יִשְׂרָאֵל פְּרִי יְשַׁוֶּה לוֹ.** יִשְׂרָאֵל דּוֹמִין לְגֶפֶן הַמַּשִּׁיר כָּל פִּרְיוֹ הַטּוֹב, כָּךְ עָזְבוּ אוֹתִי שֶׁאֲנִי פְּרִי הַטּוֹב וְהַשָּׁוֶה לוֹ:


**Israel is a vine devoid of fruit fitting for it** Israel resembles a vine that casts off all its good fruit. So did they forsake Me, Who is the good and fitting fruit for them.


###### Rashi on Hosea 10:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:1:2)


**פְּרִי יְשַׁוֶּה לוֹ. פְּרִי** אֲשֶׁר **יִשְׁוֶה לוֹ** וְיִיטַב לוֹ:


**fruit fitting for it** The fruit that is fitting for them and good for them.


###### Rashi on Hosea 10:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:1:3)


**כְּרוֹב לְפִרְיוֹ הִרְבָּה לַמִּזְבְּחוֹת.** כְּשֶׁהִשְׁפַּעְתִּי לָהֶם הַטּוֹבָה וְהֵם הִרְבּוּ לַעֲשׂוֹת עֲגָלִים לַמִּזְבְּחוֹת. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **גֶּפֶן בּוֹקֵק יִשְׂרָאֵל** – פְּרִי מַעֲשָׂיו גָּרַם לוֹ, **פְּרִי יְשַׁוֶּה לוֹ** – יוֹעִיל לוֹ לִהְיוֹת בּוֹקֵק, כִּי עָשׂוּ הַמִּזְבְּחוֹת וְהוֹעִילוּ לִכְנִיסִיָּה שֶׁלָּהֶן לְרָעָה, וְזֶהוּ לְשׁוֹן הַתַּרְגּוּם:


**When I increased their good, they increased for the altars** When I lavished good upon them, they made many calves for the altars. Another explanation **Israel is a plundered vine;** the produce of their deeds brought it about to them. פְּרִי יְשַׁוֶּה לוֹ means ‘will avail them’ to be plundered, for they made the altars and availed their nation for evil. This is the wording of the Targum.


###### Rashi on Hosea 10:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:1:4)


**הֵטִיבוּ מַצֵּבוֹת.** הִרְבּוּ מַצֵּבוֹת:


**they increased pillars** Heb. הֵטִיבוּ מַצֵבוֹת.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Hosea 10:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:2:1)


**חָלַק לִבָּם.** מִמֶּנִּי:


**Their heart has parted** from Me.


###### Rashi on Hosea 10:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:2:2)


**עַתָּה יֶאְשָׁמוּ.** הוּא הַדָּבָר אֲשֶׁר **יַעֲרֹף מִזְבְּחוֹתָם** – יַשְׁחִית. לְשׁוֹן ״וְעָרְפוּ שָׁם״ (דברים כא:ד), ״וַעֲרַפְתּוֹ״ (שמות יג:יג). יִשְׁבֹּר צַוָּארָם. דָּבָר אַחֵר: לְפִי מִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה, גָּדוֹל הַשָּׁלוֹם, שֶׁאֲפִלּוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל עָבְדוּ עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה וְשָׁלוֹם בֵּינֵיהֶם, אֵין הַשָּׂטָן מְקַטְרֵג בֵּינֵיהֶם, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: ״חֲבוּר עֲצַבִּים אֶפְרַיִם, הַנַּח לוֹ״ (לעיל ד:יז). וְשָׂנוּי הַמַּחֲלֹקֶת, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: **חָלַק לִבָּם עַתָּה יֶאְשָׁמוּ** – יֵשׁ פִּתְחוֹן פֶּה לַשָּׂטָן לְקַטְרֵג.


**now they shall be desolate**. That is the thing that shall demolish (וְעָרְפוּ) thier altars, i.e., it will destroy them. This is an expression similar to (Deut. 21:4) “and they shall dehead (יַעֲרֹף)”; (Ex. 13:13) “and you shall behead it (וְעֲרַפְתּוֹ).” He will break their neck. Another explanation according to Midrash Aggadah: Peace is great, for, even if Israel worships idols, but there is peace among them, Satan does not bring charges against them, as it is said: (above 4:17) “Ephraim is joined: although he worships idols, let him alone.” Strife, however, is hated, as it is said: “Their heart has parted; now they will be guilty.” Satan can open his mouth to bring charges. [from unknown Midrashic source]



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Hosea 10:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:3:1)


**כִּי עַתָּה.** בְּבֹא עֲלֵיהֶם הָרָעָה, **יֹאמְרוּ אֵין לָנוּ מֶלֶךְ,** מְלָכֵינוּ אֲשֶׁר בָּטַחְנוּ בּוֹ לֵאמֹר שֶׁיֵּצֵא בָּרִאשׁוֹנָה וְיִלְחַם אֶת מִלְחֲמוֹתֵינוּ (שמואל א ח:כ) אֵין מוֹעִיל לָנוּ, כְּלוּם:


**For now** when evil befalls them. **they shall say, We have no king,** Our king, upon whom we relied, saying, “He will go forth at our head and will wage our wars,” is of no avail to us.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Hosea 10:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:4:1)


**אָלוֹת שָׁוְא.** הִשָּׁבַע לַשֶּׁקֶר. ׳אָלוֹת׳ כְּמוֹ ׳כָּרוֹת׳, לְשׁוֹן הוֹוֶה. **כָּרוֹת בְּרִית** לַעֲבוֹדָה זָרָה שֶׁל מלֶךְ, לְפִיכָךְ יִפְרַח עֲלֵיהֶם מִשְׁפָּט יִסּוּרִין וּפֻרְעָנוּת **כָּרֹאשׁ** שֶׁהוּא עֵשֶׂב מַר הַפּוֹרֵחַ **עַל תַּלְמֵי שָׂדָי**. וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגִּם **עַל תַּלְמֵי שָׂדָי** בַּעֲוֹן שֶׁמַּסִּיגִין גְּבוּלֵי שָׂדוֹת:


**swearing falsely** Heb. אָלוֹת שָוְא, swearing falsely. אָלוֹת is, in construction, like כָּרֹת, a present tense. **forming a covenant** with pagan worship. Therefore, judgment of torments and retribution shall spring up upon them. **like hemlock** which is a bitter grass, springing up on the furrows of the field. And Jonathan renders: **on the furrows of the field** for the iniquity that they move back the boundaries of the field.


###### Rashi on Hosea 10:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:4:2)


**תַּלְמֵי שָׂדַי** – מַעֲנִית הַמַּחֲרֵשָׁה קָרוּי ׳תֶּלֶם׳. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **עַל תַּלְמֵי שָׂדַי** – שֶׁהֵם עוֹשִׂים שָׁם מִזְבְּחוֹתָם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: ״גַּם מִזְבְּחוֹתָם כְּגַלִּים עַל תַּלְמֵי שָׂדָי״ (לקמן יב:יב), שָׁם יִפְרַח עֲלֵיהֶם מִשְׁפַּט עֲוֹנָם. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **אָלוֹת שָׁוְא** – כָּל בְּרִית שֶׁהֵם כּוֹרְתִים זֶה עִם זֶה עוֹבְרִים עָלָיו. **וּפָרַח כָּרֹאשׁ מִשְׁפָּט עַל תַּלְמֵי שָׂדַי** – כָּרֹאשׁ שֶׁהוּא פּוֹרֵחַ עַל תַּלְמֵי שָׂדַי, שֶׁהוּא עֵשֶׂב מַר, כָּךְ מִשְׁפְּטֵיהֶם פּוֹרְחִים וְגְדֵלִים תַּמְרוּרִים לַעֲנִיִּים וְאֶבְיוֹנִים. וְאַף עָמוֹס אָמַר כֵּן: ״כִּי הֲפַכְתֶּם לְלַעֲנָה מִשְׁפָּט״ (עמוס ו:יב):


**the furrows** Heb. תַּלְמֵי. The furrow of a plowshare is called תֶּלֶם. Another explanation: **on the furrows of the field** where they erect their altars, as it is said (below 12:12): “like heaps on the furrows of the field.” There the judgment for their iniquity shall spring up on them. Another explanation: **swearing falsely** Every covenant they form with one another they break. **and...shall spring up like hemlock** which springs up on the furrows of the field, which is a bitter grass, so do their judgments spring up and bitterness grows for the poor and needy. Amos, too, says: (6:12) “For you have turned judgment into hemlock.”



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Hosea 10:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:5:1)


**לְעֶגְלוֹת בֵּית אָוֶן וגו׳.** לְפֻרְעָנוּת הָעֲתִידָה לָבֹא עַל עֲגָלִים שֶׁבְּבֵית אֵל (מלכים א יב:לב), שֶׁהִיא נִקְרֵאת בֵּית אָוֶן בְּסֵפֶר יְהוֹשֻׁעַ (יהושע ז:ב):


**Because of the calves of Beth-aven etc.** Because of the retribution destined to befall the calves in Bethel, called Beth-aven in the Book of Joshua (7:2).


###### Rashi on Hosea 10:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:5:2)


**יָגוּרוּ.** יִרְאוּ מִמֶּנּוּ שְׁכֵנָיו שֶׁבְּשׁוֹמְרוֹן, לָמָּה? כִּי אָבַל עָלָיו עַמּוֹ, וּכְמָרָיו אֲשֶׁר עָלָיו יָגִילוּ תָּמִיד עַתָּה יְאַבְּלוּ עַל כְּבוֹדוֹ כִּי גָלָה:


**shall be frightened** Heb. יָגוּרוּ. Its neighbors who were in Samaria shall be frightened over it. Why? For its people mourn over it, and its priests, who would constantly rejoice over it will not mourn over its glory, for it has been exiled.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Hosea 10:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:6:1)


**גַּם אוֹתוֹ לְאַשּׁוּר יוּבָל.** זֶה הָיָה בִּשְׁנַת שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה לְאָחָז ״וַיָּעַר ה׳ אֶת רוּחַ פּוּל מֶלֶךְ אַשּׁוּר וַיַּגְלֵם לָרְאוּבֵנִי וְלַגָּדִי וגו׳״ (דברי הימים א ה:כו) וְנָטַל עֶגְלֵי הַזָּהָב אֲשֶׁר בְּבֵית אֵל וְהָלַךְ לוֹ, לְקַיֵּם מַה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר **גַּם אוֹתוֹ לְאַשּׁוּר יוּבָל**:


**That too shall be carried off to Assyria** This took place in the twelfth year of Ahaz, “And the Lord (sic) aroused the desire of Pul king of Assyria...and they exiled the Reubenites and the Gadites etc.” (I Chron. 5:26), and he took the golden calves that were in Bethel and went away, to fulfill what was said: “That too shall be carried off to Assyria.” [from Seder Olam ch. 22]


###### Rashi on Hosea 10:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:6:2)


**מִנְחָה לְמֶלֶךְ יָרֵב.** זֶה סַנְחֵרִיב:


**a gift to King Yareb** This is Sennacherib.


###### Rashi on Hosea 10:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:6:3)


**בָּשְׁנָה אֶפְרַיִם יִקַּח.** בֹּשֶׁת יִקַּח לוֹ יָרָבְעָם, שֶׁהוּא מִשֵּׁבֶט אֶפְרַיִם, שֶׁהֶעֱמִיד לוֹ עֵגֶל זֶה לֶאֱלוֹהַּ (מלכים א יב:כח-כט):


**Ephraim shall take shame** Heb. בָּשְׁנָה. Jeroboam, who was from the tribe of Ephraim, shall take shame for himself, that he erected this calf for a deity.


###### Rashi on Hosea 10:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:6:4)


**וַיֵּבוֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵל** מֵאוֹתָהּ עֵצָה שֶׁנּוֹעֲצוּ לַעֲשׂוֹתָם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (מלכים א יב:כח): ״וַיִּוָּעַץ הַמֶּלֶךְ וַיַּעַשׂ שְׁנֵי עֶגְלֵי זָהָב״:


**and Israel shall be ashamed** of that counsel that they took counsel to make them, as it is said: (I Kings 12:28) “The king took counsel and made two golden calves.”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Hosea 10:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:7:1)


**נִדְמֶה.** נִשְׁתַּתֵּק מֶלֶךְ שׁוֹמְרוֹן, וַהֲרֵי הוּא כְּקֶצֶף שֶׁעַל פְּנֵי הַמַּיִם, שֶׁהוּא אושקמ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**The king of Samaria is silenced** Heb. נִדְמֶה. The king of Samaria is silenced, and he is like foam on the surface of the water, which is eskoume (ecume) in Old French.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Hosea 10:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:8:1)


**בָּמוֹת אָוֶן.** בָּמוֹת בֵּית אֵל:


**The high places of Aven** i.e., the high places of Bethel.


###### Rashi on Hosea 10:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:8:2)


**קוֹץ וְדַרְדַּר יַעֲלֶה עַל מִזְבְּחוֹתָם.** שֶׁגָּלוּ עוֹבְדֵיהֶן וְאֵין עוֹד פּוֹנֶה לָהֶם:


**thorns and thistles shall come up on their altars** for their worshippers have gone into exile, and no one turns to them anymore.


###### Rashi on Hosea 10:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:8:3)


**וְאָמְרוּ** יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**and they shall say** i.e., Israel shall say.


###### Rashi on Hosea 10:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:8:4)


**לֶהָרִים כַּסּוּנוּ.** שֶׁלֹּא יִרְאוּנוּ אוֹיְבֵינוּ בְּבָשְׁתֵּנוּ:


**to the mountains, Cover us up** lest our enemies see our shame.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Hosea 10:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:9:1)


**מִימֵי הַגִּבְעָה חָטָאת יִשְׂרָאֵל.** חָטָאת זֶה נָקוּד רָפֵי, שֶׁהוּא לְשׁוֹן נְקֵבָה וּלְשֶׁעָבַר, כְּמוֹ: עָשָׂת, בָּאת, אָזְלַת יָד. מִימֵי גִּבְעַת בִּנְיָמִין חָטָאת יִשְׂרָאֵל, לְפִי שֶׁהָיָה מַעֲשֵׂה פִּלֶגֶשׁ בַּגִּבְעָה בִּימֵי עָתְנִיאֵל בֶּן קְנַז, שֶׁהָיָה רִאשׁוֹן לַשּׁוֹפְטִים בִּימֵי כּוּשַׁן רִשְׁעָתַיִם, וּבְיָמָיו הָיָה פֶּסֶל מִיכָה, וְכָל זֶה מָצִינוּ בְּסֵדֶר עוֹלָם:


**Since the days of Gibeah, you have sinned, O Israel!** Heb. חָטָאתָ. This word חָטָאת is punctuated weakly, since it is of the feminine gender and of the past tense. Comp. (Lev. 25:21) וְעָשָת, “and it shall produce”; (Gen. 16:8) בָאת, “have you come”; (Deut. 32:36) אָזְלַת, “their power is gone.” From the days of Gibeah of Benjamin, Israel sinned. Since the incident of the concubine in Gibeah (Jud. 19) took place during the time of Othniel the son of Kenaz, who was the first of the judges, at the time of Cushan-rishathaim, and in whose time the image of Micah existed, and all this we find in Seder Olam (ch. 12).


###### Rashi on Hosea 10:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:9:2)


**שָׁם עָמָדוּ.** בְּאוֹתָהּ מִדָּה אָחֲזוּ לָהֶם מֵאָז:


**There they remained** They adhered to that trait since then.


###### Rashi on Hosea 10:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:9:3)
