**לֹא תַשִּׂיגֵם בַּגִּבְעָה מִלְחָמָה וְגוֹ׳.** וּלְפִי שֶׁהָיָה עֲוֹן עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה בִּידֵיהֶם וְלֹא הִקְפִּידוּ עָלָיו, לְכָךְ לֹא הִצְלִיחָה יָדָם **בְּמִלְחֶמֶת הַגִּבְעָה** עַל בְּנֵי בִּנְיָמִין (שופטים כ). וּקְרָאָם **בְּנֵי עַלְוָה** – בְּנֵי גַּאֲוָה, שֶׁעָשׂוּ עַצְמָם עֶלְיוֹנִים וְלֹא שָׁמְעוּ בְּקוֹל אֲחֵיהֶם לָתֵת אֶת בְּנֵי הַגִּבְעָה (שופטים כ:יג). וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם עַל שֶׁשָּׁאֲלוּ לָהֶם מֶלֶךְ וְהִמְלִיכוּ אֶת שָׁאוּל, וְאֵינוֹ מִתְיַשֵּׁב לִי אַחַר לְשׁוֹן הָעִבְרִית, וְגַם שְׁאָרֵי חִבּוּר הַפָּרָשָׁה נוֹפֵל עַל לָשׁוֹן הָרִאשׁוֹן:


**the battle...did not overtake them in Gibeah** Since the iniquity of idolatry was with them and they were not concerned about it, they were not victorious in the battle of Gibeah against the Benjaminites. Scripture calls them בְּנֵי עַלְוָה, lit. sons of haughtiness, who made themselves superior (עֶלְיוֹנִים) and did not heed their brethren to deliver the people of Gibeah. Jonathan, however, translates this in reference to their request for a king and their crowning Saul. However this does not appear to me to follow the Hebrew wording. Moreover, the context fits the former interpretation.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Hosea 10:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:10:1)


**בְּאַוָּתִי וְאֶסֳּרֵם.** לְפִי רְצוֹנִי תָּמִיד יִסַּרְתִּים מִשּׁוֹפֵט לְשׁוֹפֵט, וּמְסַרְתִּים בְּיַד שׁוֹסֵיהֶם (שופטים ב:טז-יט):


**With My will, I chastised them** According to My will, I always chastised them from judge to judge, and I delivered them into the hands of their plunderers.


###### Rashi on Hosea 10:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:10:2)


**וְאֻסְּפוּ עֲלֵיהֶם עַמִּים בְּאָסְרָם לִשְׁתֵּי עוֹנֹתָם.** לְפִי שֶׁדִּמָּה אוֹתָם לְעֶגְלָה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר אַחֲרָיו: ״אֶפְרַיִם עֶגְלָה מְלֻמָּדָה״ (הושע י:יא), לְכָךְ דִּמָּה יִסּוּרֵיהֶם לְפָרָה שֶׁמְּיַגְּעִין אוֹתָהּ בַּחֲרִישָׁה, וְאָסְרוּהָ בְּעֹל הַמַּחֲרֵשָׁה, וְסִימְלוֹנֵי הָעֹל נוֹקְבִים וְיוֹרְדִים אֶחָד מִכָּאן וְאֶחָד מִכָּאן אֵצֶל שְׁנֵי עֵינֵי הַשּׁוֹר. וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״כְּמֵיסַר פַּדְנָא עַל תַּרְתֵּין עֵינוֹהִי״. פַּדְנָא – צֶמֶד בָּקָר.


**and nations shall gather about them, when they bind them to their two eyes** Since Hosea compares them to a heifer as stated further: “Ephraim is a goaded heifer,” he, therefore, compared their chastisement to a cow, which they tire with plowing and they bind it to the yoke of the plowshare, and the pegs of the yoke penetrate it, [i.e., they penetrate the yoke,] one on this side and one on that side, next to the ox’s two eyes. And so did Jonathan render: as one hinds a “padna” on its two eyes. “Padna” is a yoke of oxen.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Hosea 10:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:11:1)


**וְאֶפְרַיִם עֶגְלָה מְלֻמָּדָה.** פירפויינט״א בְּלַעַ״ז, עֲשׂוּיָה חֲבוּרוֹת חֲבוּרוֹת בְּמַלְמַד הַבָּקָר שֶׁקּוֹרִין דישגוֹלויי״ן בְּלַעַ״ז. כְּלוֹמַר, הַרְבֵּה יִסּוּרִין הֵבֵאתִי עָלֶיהָ וַהֲרֵי הִיא לֹא נִכְנְעָה, וְתָמִיד אוֹהֶבֶת לָדוּשׁ בַּתְּבוּאָה מְקוֹם מַאֲכָל וּמִשְׁמָן, וְלֹא נִכְנְעָה לִהְיוֹת חוֹרֶשֶׁת. כְּלוֹמַר, לֹא נִכְנְעוּ לַתּוֹרָה אֶלָּא אַחַר שְׁרִירוּת לִבָּם (ירמיה ט:יג):


**And Ephraim is a goaded heifer** Heb. מְלֻמָּדָה, porpojjnte in O.F. punctured, wounded in many places with the oxgoad, which is called agojjlon in O.F. I.e., many chastisements have I brought upon her, yet she has not humbled herself, and he always loves to thresh the grain in a place of food and fat, and she did not subject herself to be plowing, i.e., they did not subject themselves to My Torah, but follow the vision of their heart.


###### Rashi on Hosea 10:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:11:2)


**אֹהַבְתִּי.** יוֹ״ד יְתֵרָה, כְּמוֹ (בראשית לא:לט) ״גְּנֻבְתִי יוֹם״:


**that loves** Heb. אֹהַבְתִּי. The “yud” is superfluous. Comp. (Gen. 31: 39) “stolen by day (גְּנֻבְתִי).”


###### Rashi on Hosea 10:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:11:3)


**וַאֲנִי עָבַרְתִּי עַל טוּב צַוָּארָהּ.** לְפִיכָךְ אֲנִי מֵבִיא עֲלֵיהֶם מְלָכִים שֶׁיָּתִישׁוּ אֶת כֹּחָם:


**and I passed over her fair neck** Therefore, I bring upon them kings who will weaken their strength.


###### Rashi on Hosea 10:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:11:4)


**אַרְכִּיב אֶפְרַיִם יַחֲרוֹשׁ יְהוּדָה וְגוֹ׳.** אִם תִּרְצוּ שֶׁ**אַרְכִּיב אֶפְרַיִם** עַל עוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת, **יַחֲרוֹשׁ יְהוּדָה** וִישַׂדֵּד לוֹ (יַעֲקֹב) לְעַצְמוֹ חֲרִישָׁה שֶׁל מַעֲשִׂים טוֹבִים, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוּא מְפָרֵשׁ וְאוֹמֵר: ״נִירוּ לָכֶם נִיר וְגוֹ׳״ (הושע י:יב):


**I will cause Ephraim to ride, Judah shall plow etc.** If you wish that I would cause Ephraim to ride upon the nations, Judah shall plow, and Jacob יְשַּׂדֶד, shall break his clods with a plowing of good deeds, as Hosea explains and says: plow yourselves a plowing etc.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Hosea 10:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:12:1)


**זִרְעוּ לָכֶם לִצְדָקָה.** אֲבָל אַתֶּם ״חֲרַשְׁתֶּם רֶשַׁע וְגוֹ׳״ (הושע י:יג), לְפִיכָךְ ״וְקָאם שָׁאוֹן בְּעַמֶּךָ״ (הושע י:יד):


**Sow righteousness for yourselves** But you have plowed wickedness etc.; therefore, a tumult shall rise in your people.


###### Rashi on Hosea 10:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:12:2)


**נִירוּ לָכֶם נִיר.** עִסְקוּ בַּתּוֹרָה, וּמִשָּׁם תִּלְמְדוּ לָלֶכֶת בְּדֶרֶךְ טוֹבָה לְהִתְגַּבֵּר עַל יֵצֶר הָרָע, כְּזֶה שֶׁהוּא נָר אֶת הַשָּׂדֶה לְהַפּוֹךְ שָׁרְשֵׁי הָעֲשָׂבִים הַמַּכְחִישִׁים בַּתְּבוּאָה מִימוֹת הַחַמָּה כַּמָּה יָמִים לִפְנֵי הַזֶּרַע, וְעֵת תִּקְבְּעוּ לִדְרוֹשׁ אֶת ה׳ לְתַלְמוּד תּוֹרָה:


**plow yourselves a plowing** Engage in the Torah and from there you will learn to go on the good way, to overpower temptation, like a person who plows the field to turn over the roots of grasses which sap the strength of the grain in the summer many days prior to sowing, and you shall set aside a time to seek the Lord, i.e., for the study of the Torah.


###### Rashi on Hosea 10:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:12:3)


**עַד יָבֹא וְיוֹרֶה צֶדֶק לָכֶם.** כְּשֶׁתִּהְיוּ יְגֵעִים בָּהּ הוּא יָבִין אֶתְכֶם לְהוֹדִיעֲכֶם צְפוּנוֹתֶיהָ בְּצֶדֶק. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **נִירוּ לָכֶם** נִיר, עֲשׂוּ מַעֲשִׂים טוֹבִים קֹדֶם שֶׁיָּבֹא עֲלֵיכֶם הַלַּחַץ, וְזֶה יִגְרֹם לָכֶם שֶׁתִּתְקַבֵּל תְּפִלַּתְכֶם בְּעֵת הַצֹּרֶךְ, וְאָז יִהְיֶה עֵת רָאוּי לָכֶם לִדְרוֹשׁ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא בְּכָל צָרְכֵיכֶם, כִּי אָז תִּשָּׁמַע צַעֲקַתְכֶם וְיוֹרֶה צֶדֶק לָכֶם וְיַמְטִיר צְדָקָה לָכֶם, כְּמוֹ ״יָרָה בַיָּם״ (שמות טו:ד):


**until He comes and instructs you in righteousness** When you toil in it, He will give you to understand to let you know its secrets with righteousness. Another explanation: **Plow yourselves a plowing** Do good deeds before oppression comes upon you, and that will cause for you that your prayer will be accepted in time of necessity, and then it will be a time fit for you to seek the Holy One, blessed he He, for all your necessities, for then your cry will be heard, and He will cast down (יוֹרֶה) charity to you; i.e., He will rain down charity to you. Comp. (Ex. 15:3) “He cast (יָרָה) into the sea.”



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Hosea 10:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:13:1)


**אֲכַלְתֶּם פְּרִי כָחַשׁ.** ״אִיתְקַבַּלְתּוּן תּוּשְׁלָמַת עוֹבָדֵיכוֹן״ (תרגום יונתן):


**you have eaten fruit of lies** You have received the retribution for your deeds.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Hosea 10:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:14:1)


**וְקָאם שָׁאוֹן.** קוֹל עֲנוֹת נוּסוּ נוּסוּ:


**And a tumult shall rise in your peoples** i.e., a voice shouting, “Flee! Flee!”


###### Rashi on Hosea 10:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:14:2)


**כְּשֹׁד שַׁלְמַן בֵּית אַרְבֵּאל.** כְּבוֹזְזִים הַבָּאִים עַל עַם יוֹשֵׁב בְּשָׁלוֹם פִּתְאוֹם עַל יַד מַאֲרָב, שֶׁלֹּא נִזְהֲרוּ בָּם לִבְרֹחַ מִפְּנֵיהֶם, וְשׁוֹדְדִים אֶת הַכֹּל:


**as the plunder of a peaceful people by an ambush** Like plunderers who come upon a tranquil people suddenly with an ambush, for they did not beware of them to flee from before them, and they plunder everything.


###### Rashi on Hosea 10:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:14:3)


**בֵּית אַרְבְּאֵל.** יוֹנָתָן תִּרְגְּמוֹ ׳בֵּית מַאֲרָב׳, וְאָלֶ״ף וְלָמֶ״ד יְתֵרָה בּוֹ, כְּמוֹ ׳יִזְרְעֶאל׳ שֶׁאֵינוֹ שֵׁם עִיר, כְּגוֹן (לעיל ב) ״יַעֲנוּ אֶת יִזְרְעֶאל״, וּקְרָאוֹ בִּלְשׁוֹן שֵׁם עִיר. אַף כָּאן קָרָא אוֹתוֹ כְּמִין שֵׁם עִיר, וְכֵן חִבְּרוֹ מְנַחֵם, אֲבָל דּוֹנָשׁ פּוֹתְרוֹ שֵׁם מָקוֹם:


**an ambush** Heb. בֵּית אַרְבֵּאל. Jonathan renders: a place of ambush (בֵּית מַאֲרָב), and the “aleph” “lammed” are superfluous. Comp. יִזְרְעֶאל, which is not the name of a city, e.g., (above 2: 24) “And they shall answer Jezreel,” and he called it with the expression of a name. Here, too, he called it as a sort of name. In this manner, Menahem (Machbereth Menahem p. 34) classified it, but Dunash (Teshuvoth Dunash p. 49) interprets it as a placename.


###### Rashi on Hosea 10:14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:14:4)


**רֻטָּשָׁה.** נִבְקְעָה, וְכֵן ״נְעָרִים תְּרַטַּשְׁנָה״ (ישעיהו יג:יח), ״וְעוֹלְלֵיהֶם יְרֻטָּשׁוּ״ (שם). לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: **רֻטָּשָׁה** עֲזוּבָה, כְּמוֹ ׳נֻטָּשָׁה׳.


**was dashed to pieces** Heb. רֻטָּשָׁה, split. Comp. (Isa. 13:18) “shall dash (תְּרַטַשְנָא) youths”; (ibid. v. 16) “and their babes shall be dashed (יְרֻטָשׁוּ) .” Another explanation of רֻטָּשָׁה is “abandoned,” like נֻטָּשָׁה.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Hosea 10:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:15:1)


**כָּכָה עָשָׂה לָכֶם בֵּית אֵל.** כָּל הַפֻּרְעָנוּת הַזֶּה גָּרַם לָכֶם הָעֵגֶל שֶׁבְּבֵית אֵל (מלכים א יב:כח-כט):


**So has Bethel done to you** The calf in Bethel has brought about all this retribution upon you.


###### Rashi on Hosea 10:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:15:2)


**רָעַת רָעַתְכֶם.** רָעַת רִשְׁעֲכֶם, עֲבֵירוֹת זְדוֹנְכֶם:


**the evil of your wickedness** Heb., lit. the evil of your evil. Because of the evil of your wickedness, the sins of your evil intention.


###### Rashi on Hosea 10:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/10:15:3)


**בַּשַּׁחַר נִדְמֹה נִדְמָה.** נִרְדַּם כְּאִלּוּ בַּלַּיְלָה, כְּלוֹמַר אֵין בּוֹ כֹּחַ. וְלָמָּה כָּל זֹאת? כִּי בִּהְיוֹת יִשְׂרָאֵל נַעַר וָאֹהֲבֵהוּ (הושע יא:א) מִנּוֹעַר מִכָּל טוֹב:


**at dawn the king of Israel has been silenced** Heb. נִדְמֹה נִדְמָה. He has sunk into a deep sleep as though it were night; i.e. he has no strength. Now why has all this come about? For when Israel was young, devoid of all good, I loved him.



## Chapter 11


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Hosea 11:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:1:1)


**וּמִמִּצְרַיִם קָרָאתִי לִבְנִי.** עַל יְדֵי נְבִיאַי לְהִדָּבֵק בְּתוֹרָתִי, וְהֵם מֶה עָשׂוּ (שמות ד:כב):


**and from Egypt I called My son** through My prophets to cleave to My Torah. But what did they do?...



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Hosea 11:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:2:1)


**קָרְאוּ לָהֶם כֵּן הָלְכוּ מִפְּנֵיהֶם.** כָּל מַה שֶׁהַנְּבִיאִים קוֹרְאִים לָהֶם לְלַמְּדָם דֶּרֶךְ הַטּוֹבָה, כֵּן הֵם הָפְכוּ עֹרֶף לִבְרֹחַ מִפְּנֵיהֶם:


**The more they called to them, the more they went away from them** As much as the prophets call to teach them the good way, so did they turn their backs to run away from them.


###### Rashi on Hosea 11:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:2:2)


**לַבְּעָלִים יְזַבֵּחוּ.** דֶּרֶךְ הַמִּקְרָא לְדַבֵּר כֵּן, כְּמוֹ ״כְּרֻבָּם כֵּן חָטְאוּ לִי״ (הושע ד:ז), וְ״כַאֲשֶׁר יְעַנּוּ אוֹתוֹ כֵּן יִרְבֶּה״ (שמות א:יב):


**and they would slaughter sacrifices to the baalim.** It is customary for Scripture to speak in this manner. Comp. (above 4:7) “The more they have increased, the more they have sinned against Me”; (Ex. 1:12) “And the more they afflicted them, the more they increased.”



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Hosea 11:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:3:1)


**וְאָנֹכִי תִרְגַּלְתִּי לְאֶפְרַיִם קָחָם וגו׳.** שָׁלַחְתִּי לִפְנֵיהֶם מַנְהִיג הַמַּרְגִּילָם בְּנַחַת (שמות יג:כא):


**But I sent to train Ephraim, he took them etc.** I sent a leader before them who would train them gently.


###### Rashi on Hosea 11:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:3:2)


**קָחָם עַל זְרוֹעֹתָיו.** זֶה מֹשֶׁה, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בּוֹ (במדבר יא:יב): ״כַּאֲשֶׁר יִשָּׂא הָאוֹמֵן אֶת הַיּוֹנֵק״:


**he took them on his arms** This is Moses, concerning whom it is stated (Num. 11:12): “As the nursing father carries the sucking child.”


###### Rashi on Hosea 11:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:3:3)


**תִרְגַּלְתִּי.** כְּמוֹ ׳הִרְגַּלְתִּי׳, וְאֵין לוֹ דִּמְיוֹן:


The form תִּרְגַּלְתִּי is like הִרגַּלְתִּי, I trained, or accustomed, and there is no similar form in Scripture.


###### Rashi on Hosea 11:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:3:4)


**וְלֹא יָדְעוּ כִּי רְפָאתִים.** יוֹדְעִין הָיוּ, אֶלָּא שֶׁדָּשׁוּ בְּעָקֵב, עָשׂוּ עַצְמָם כְּלֹא יוֹדְעִים. וְאַגָּדָה בְּמִדְרַשׁ רַבִּי תַּנְחוּמָא (תנחומא, ויחי ו) דָּרְשׁוּ כְּלַפֵּי בְּנֵי יוֹסֵף: כְּשֶׁהֱבִיאָם לִפְנֵי יַעֲקֹב לְבָרְכָם, רָאָה יַעֲקֹב רְשָׁעִים עֲתִידִים לָצֵאת מֵאֶפְרַיִם – יָרָבְעָם וְאַחְאָב, וְנִסְתַּלְּקָה מִמֶּנּוּ רוּחַ הַקֹּדֶשׁ, ״וַיֹּאמֶר מִי אֵלֶּה״ (בראשית מח:ח). בִּקֵּשׁ יוֹסֵף רַחֲמִים וְשָׁרְתָה רוּחַ הַקֹּדֶשׁ עַל יַעֲקֹב וּבֵרְכָם. זֶהוּ **וְאָנֹכִי תִרְגַּלְתִּי** רוּחִי עַל יַעֲקֹב לְצֹרֶךְ אֶפְרַיִם, **וְלָקְחָם עַל זְרוֹעוֹתָיו**:


**but they did not know that I healed them** They, in fact, knew, but they trod with their heels; they pretended not to know. The Aggadah in the Midrash of Rabbi Tanhuma (Gen. 48:1) expounds it in reference to the sons of Joseph. When he brought them before Jacob to bless them, Jacob perceived wicked men destined to come from Ephraim, viz. Jeroboam and Ahab, and the Holy Spirit left him, “and he said: Who are these?” Joseph prayed for mercy, and the Holy Spirit rested on Jacob and he blessed them. This is the meaning of: And I trained My spirit upon Jacob for Ephraim’s benefit, and he took them on his arms.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Hosea 11:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:4:1)


**בְּחַבְלֵי אָדָם.** הָיִיתִי מוֹשְׁכָם תָּמִיד בַּחֲבָלִים רַכִּים אֲשֶׁר יִמְשֹׁךְ בָּהֶם אָדָם אֶת בְּנוֹ, כְּלוֹמַר בִּמְשִׁיכַת רַחֲמִים, וְכֵן תַּרְגּוּם יוֹנָתָן:


**with cords of a man** I constantly drew them with tender cords with which a man draws his son; i.e., drawing mercifully. Jonathan, too, rendered it in this manner.


###### Rashi on Hosea 11:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:4:2)


**וָאֶהְיֶה לָהֶם כִּמְרִימֵי עֹל עַל לְחֵיהֶם.** כְּאִכָּרִים הַטּוֹבִים הַמְּרִימִים עֹל בִּידֵיהֶם עַל יְדֵי לְוָחִים לְהָקֵל מֵעַל הַפָּרָה הַחוֹרֶשֶׁת, כֵּן הָיִיתִי עִמָּם בְּכָל צָרָה לְהָקֵל מֵעֲלֵיהֶן. (ל"א סא"א) **כִּמְרִימֵי עֹל עַל לְחֵיהֶם** – כְּאִכָּרִים הַמְּרִימִים עֹל מֵעַל צַוַּאר הַשּׁוֹר עַל יְדֵי שֶׁמַּאֲרִיכִין אֶת הַכְּלוֹנָס שֶׁהָעֹל נִתַּן בְּרֹאשׁוֹ, לְהָקֵל מֵעַל הַפָּרָה הַחוֹרֶשֶׁת, כֵּן הָיִיתִי עִמָּם בְּכָל צָרָה לְהָקֵל מֵעֲלֵיהֶם. כֵּן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״כְּאִיכָּרָא טָבָא וּמוֹרִיךְ בִּלְחֵיאָתְהוֹן״.


**and I was to them like those who lift the yoke on their bridles** I.e., like the good farmers who lift the yoke with their hands through bridles to lighten it for the cow that is plowing. So was I with them in every trouble, to make it lighter for them. Another explanation: **like those who lift the yoke on their bridles** Like the farmers who lift the yoke off the neck of the cow or the ox by lengthening the peg upon which the yoke is placed, to lighten it for the plowing cow. So was I with them in every trouble to make it lighter for them. So did Jonathan render it: like a good farmer [who makes it lighter for the shoulders of the oxen and lengthens their bridles.]


###### Rashi on Hosea 11:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:4:3)


**וְעַל לְחֵיהֶם.** לְשׁוֹן לֶחִי שֶׁל מָבוֹי:


**on their bridles** Heb. אוֹכִיל, an expression of a stake of an alley.


###### Rashi on Hosea 11:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:4:4)


**וָאַט אֵלָיו אוֹכִיל.** הִטֵּיתִי לוֹ כֹּחַ לְהָכִיל עֹל הַיִּסּוּרִין, וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם עַל שֵׁם שֶׁסִּפֵּק לָהֶם מְזוֹנוֹת בַּמִּדְבָּר:


**and I extended to him the ability to bear** I extended him the strength to bear the yoke of the torments. Jonathan renders: referring to His supplying them food in the desert.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Hosea 11:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:5:1)


**לֹא יָשׁוּב אֶל אֶרֶץ מִצְרַיִם.** הִבְטַחְתִּיו ״לֹא תוֹסִיפוּ לִרְאוֹתָם עוֹד וְגוֹ׳״ (שמות יד:יג), וּמַה הוֹעִיל? הֲרֵי עַל כָּרְחוֹ **אַשּׁוּר הוּא מַלְכּוֹ**. הֲרֵי חָטְאוּ וְגָרְמוּ לָהֶם לִהְיוֹת עֲבָדִים לְאַשּׁוּר, עַל **כִּי מֵאֲנוּ לָשׁוּב**.


**He shall not return to the land of Egypt** I promised him (Ex. 14:13): “you shall no longer continue to see them etc.” Now what did it avail? Against his will, Assyria is his king. They sinned and brought about that they should be vassals to Assyria since they refused to repent.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Hosea 11:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:6:1)


**וְחָלָה חֶרֶב.** וְתָנוּחַ חֶרֶב בְּעָרָיו:


**And a sword shall rest in his cities** Heb. וְחָלָה, and a sword shall rest in his cities.


###### Rashi on Hosea 11:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:6:2)


**וְכִלְּתָה בַדָּיו וְאָכְלָה.** וְכִלְּתָה גִבּוֹרָיו וְתֹאכְלֵם:


**and it shall destroy his branches, and it shall consume** And it shall destroy his mighty men and consume them. [after Targum Jonathan]


###### Rashi on Hosea 11:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:6:3)


**מִמּוֹעֲצוֹתֵיהֶם.** נָקוּד שְׁנֵי טְעָמִים, בּוֹ הַטִּפְּחָא בּוֹ הַסִּלּוּק, לְפִי שֶׁהוּא דִּבּוּר בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ, וְכֵן ״לְהֵחַלּוֹ״ דְּ״לֹא יִטַּמָּא בַּעַל בְּעַמָּיו״ (ויקרא כא:ד), וְכֵן ״לְדֹרֹתֵיכֶם״ דְּ״שֶׁמֶן מִשְׁחַת קֹדֶשׁ״ (שמות ל:לא):


**because of their counsels** (all these came upon them.) The word מִמֹּעֲצוֹתֵיהֶם is punctuated by two accents: it contains a tipcha and it contains a silluk (sof passuk), since it is a clause by itself. Comp. (Lev. 21:4) לְהֵחַלְוֹ, “for him to become profaned,” of the verse commencing, “A husband shall not contaminate himself.” Similarly, (Ex. 30:3l) לדֹרֹתֵיכֶם, “to your generations,” of the verse dealing with the holy anointing oil.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Hosea 11:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:7:1)


**וְעַמִּי תְלוּאִים לִמְשׁוּבָתִי.** כְּשֶׁהַנְּבִיאִים מְלַמְּדִים אוֹתָם לָשׁוּב אֵלַי, תְלוּאִים הֵם אִם לָשׁוּב אִם לֹא לָשׁוּב; בְּקֹשִׁי יְשׁוּבוּן אֵלַי:


**And My people waver whether to return to Me** When the prophets teach them to return to Me, they are in doubt whether to return or not to return; it is with difficulty that they return to Me.


###### Rashi on Hosea 11:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:7:2)


**וְאֶל עַל יִקְרָאֻהוּ יַחַד לֹא יְרוֹמֵם.** וְאֶל הַדָּבָר אֲשֶׁר עָלָיו יִקְרָאוּהוּ הַנְּבִיאִים יַחַד, לֹא יְרוֹמְמוּהוּ עַמִּי וְלֹא יֵאוֹת לַעֲשׂוֹתוֹ. וְיֵשׁ מְפָרֵשׁ ״וְאֶל עַל יִקְרָאֻהוּ״ – פְּגָעִים קָשִׁים יִפְגְּעוּם, וְ׳אֶל׳ כְּמוֹ ״יֵשׁ לְאֵל יָדִי״ (בראשית לא:כט), וְאֵין זֹאת, שֶׁהֲרֵי נָקוּד בְּפַתַּח, וְאִם הָיָה וְאֵל שֵׁם דָּבָר הָיָה נָקוּד בְּצֵירֵי. וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״בְּמַרְעָא קַשְׁיָא יִתְעָרְעוּן״, עָשָׂה אֶל (סא"א: עַל) לְשׁוֹן ׳קָשֶׁה׳, וְלִי נִרְאֶה שֶׁפֵּרַשְׁתִּי כַּהֹגֶן:


**and to the matter concerning which they call them, together they do not uphold it** And to the matter concerning which the prophets call them, together My people will not uphold it and will not agree to do it. Some explain וְאֶל-עַל יִקְרָאֻהוּ to mean: Harsh happenings shall befall them. The word וְאֶל means the same as (Gen. 31:29) “My hand has power (לְאֵל),” [and the word עַל means the same as עֲלִילוֹת, deeds (Kara). But this is not so since אֶל is vowelized with a “pattah.” [I.e. with a “segol,” called “pattah katan” by the early grammarians.] Were אֶל a noun, it would be vowelized with a “tseireh,” [i.e., אֵל]. Jonathan, however, renders: A harsh happening will befall them. He interprets וְאֶל as an expression of harshness, but it appears to me that I have interpreted it properly.


###### Rashi on Hosea 11:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:7:3)


**יַחַד לֹא יְרוֹמֵם.** ״כַּחֲדָא לָא יְהַכּוּן בְּקוֹמָא זְקוּפָא״ (תרגום יונתן):


**together they do not uphold [it]** [Jonathan renders:] Together they shall not walk erect.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Hosea 11:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:8:1)


**אֲמַגֶּנְךָ.** אֶמְסָרְךָ בְּיַד אוֹיְבֶיךָ, כְּמוֹ ״אֲשֶׁר מִגֵּן צָרֶיךָ״ (בראשית יד:כ):


**deliver you** Heb. אֲמַגֶּנְךָ. Comp. (Gen. 14:20) “Who delivered (מִגֵן) your adversaries.”


###### Rashi on Hosea 11:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:8:2)


**נִכְמְרוּ.** נִתְחַמְּמוּ, לְשׁוֹן אֲרַמִּי, וְכֵן ״עוֹרֵנוּ כְּתַנּוּר נִכְמָרוּ״ (איכה ה:י):


**My remorse has been kindled** Heb. נִכְרְמוּ, are heated. This is an Aramaic expression. Comp. (Lam. 5:10) “Our skin is heated (נִכְמָרוּ) like an oven.”



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Hosea 11:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:9:1)


**לֹא אָשׁוּב.** מִדִּבְרֵי הַטּוֹב אֲשֶׁר אָמַרְתִּי (ויקרא כו:מד) ״לֹא מְאַסְתִּים וְלֹא גְעַלְתִּים״ לִהְיוֹת מַשְׁחִית אֶת אֶפְרָיִם:


**I will not return** from My good word, which I said. (Lev. 26:44) “I have not rejected them nor have I abhorred them,” to destroy Ephraim.


###### Rashi on Hosea 11:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:9:2)


**כִּי אֵל אָנֹכִי.** הַמְקַיֵּם דְּבַר טוֹבָתִי, וְאֵין מִדָּתִי לְהִנָּחֵם עַל הַטּוֹב:


**for I am God** Who keeps His favorable word, and I do not retract the good.


###### Rashi on Hosea 11:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:9:3)


**בְּקִרְבְּךָ קָדוֹשׁ וְלֹא אָבוֹא בְּעִיר.** אַחֶרֶת, כְּבָר הִבְטַחְתִּי לְהַשְׁרוֹת שְׁכִינָתִי **בְּקִרְבְּךָ** בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם, וְלֹא אַשְׁרֶה אוֹתָהּ עוֹד עַל עִיר אַחֶרֶת. וְיֵשׁ מְפָרֵשׁ **בְּעִיר** לְשׁוֹן ׳שִׂנְאָה׳, כְּמוֹ (שמואל א כח:טז) ״וַיְהִי עָרֶךָ״:


**I will not enter a city** I.e., another city. I have already promised to cause My Shechinah to rest in your midst in Jerusalem, and I will never again cause it to rest on another city. Others explain it as an expression of hatred. Comp. (I Sam. 28:16) “And has become your adversary (וַיְהִי עָרֶךָ).”



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Hosea 11:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:10:1)


**כְּאַרְיֵה יִשְׁאָג.** עוֹד יִשְׁאָג לָהֶם כְּאַרְיֵה שֶׁיֵּצְאוּ מִן הַגָּלֻיּוֹת וְיֵלְכוּ אַחֲרָיו:


**He shall roar like a lion** He will yet roar to them like a lion that they leave the exile and follow Him.


###### Rashi on Hosea 11:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/11:10:2)


**וְיֶחֶרְדוּ בָנִים מִיָּם.** ״וְיִתְכַּנְּשׁוּן גָּלְוָותָא מִמַּעֲרָב״ (תרגום יונתן):


**and the children shall hasten from the west** And the exiles shall be gathered from the west. [from Jonathan]



## Chapter 12


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Hosea 12:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:1:1)


**סְבָבוּנִי.** כְּבָר בְכַחַשׁ וּבְמִרְמָה אֶפְרַיִם וּבֵית יִשְׂרָאֵל, מַלְכֵי עֲשֶׂרֶת הַשְּׁבָטִים וְכָל הָעָם, אֲבָל יְהוּדָה עוֹדֶנּוּ רָד עִם אֵל, מוֹשֵׁל עוֹד בְּיִרְאַת אֱלֹהִים. **רָד** כְּמוֹ (במדבר כד:יט) ״וְיֵרְדְּ מִיַּעֲקֹב״, עוֹד מַלְכֵיהֶם עִם הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**has surrounded Me** Ephraim and the house of Israel have already surrounded Me with lies and deceit, i.e., the kings of the Ten Tribes and all the people, but Judah still... **rules with God** Still rules with fear of God. **rules** Heb. רָד. Comp. (Num. 24:19) “And there shall rule (וְיֵרְדְּ) out of Jacob.” Their kings are still with the Holy One, blessed be He.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Hosea 12:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:2:1)


**רוֹעֶה רוּחַ.** לְשׁוֹן ׳רֵיעַ׳, מִתְחַבֵּר לְדִבְרֵי רוּחַ לְהָעֲבוֹדָה זָרָה. **וְשֶׁמֶן** שֶׁלָּהֶם לְמִצְרַיִם מוֹבִילִים לָתֵת שֹׁחַד לְמִצְרַיִם לַעֲזוֹר אוֹתִי:


**joins the wind** Heb. רֹעֵה. An expression of רֵעַ, a friend. He joins words of the wind, viz. idolatry. **and oil** Their oil they carry to Egypt to give a bribe to Egypt to aid them.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Hosea 12:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:3:1)


**וְרִיב לַה׳ עִם יְהוּדָה.** אֲלֵיהֶם הוּא מַגִּיד אֶת דִּבְרֵי רִיבוֹ אֲשֶׁר עָשׂוּ לוֹ אֲחֵיהֶם בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל, וְלֹא יִתְמְהוּ בְּפָקְדוֹ **עַל יַעֲקֹב כִּדְרָכָיו**:


**Now the Lord has a contention with Judah** He relates to them the matters of His contention which their brethren, the house of Israel, have done to Him, so that they should not wonder when He visits retribution on Jacob according to his ways.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Hosea 12:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:4:1)


**בַּבֶּטֶן עָקַב אֶת אָחִיו.** כָּל זֶה עָשִׂיתִי לוֹ, אָחֲזוֹ בְּעַקֵבוֹ סִימָן שֶׁהוּא יִהְיֶה לוֹ גְּבִיר (בראשית כז:כט):


**In the womb, he seized his brother’s heel** All this I did for him, he held him by the heel, as a sign that he would be a master over him.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Hosea 12:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:5:1)


**בָּכָה.** הַמַּלְאָךְ:


**he wept** i.e., the angel wept. [from Chullin 92a]


###### Rashi on Hosea 12:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:5:2)


**וַיִּתְחַנֶּן לוֹ.** כְּשֶׁאָמַר לוֹ ״לֹא אֲשַׁלֵּחֲךָ כִּי אִם בֵּרַכְתָּנִי״ (בראשית לב:כז), הַמַּלְאָךְ הָיָה מְבַקֵּשׁ מִמֶּנּוּ: ״הַנַּח לִי עַכְשָׁיו, סוֹפוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לִגָּלוֹת עָלֶיךָ בְּבֵית אֵל, וְשָׁם יִמְצָאֶנּוּ וְשָׁם יְדַבֵּר עִמָּנוּ, וְהוּא וַאֲנִי נַסְכִּים לְךָ עַל הַבְּרָכוֹת שֶׁבֵּרַכְךָ יִצְחָק״. וְאוֹתוֹ מַלְאָךְ שָׂרוֹ שֶׁל עֵשָׂו הָיָה (בראשית רבה עז:ג), וְהָיָה מְעַרְעֵר עַל הַבְּרָכוֹת:


**and beseeched him** When he said to him, “I will not let you go unless you bless me” (Gen. 32:26), and the angel was begging him, “Let me go now. Eventually, the Holy One, blessed be He, will reveal Himself to you in Bethel, and there you will find Him, and there He will speak with us, and He and I will agree with you concerning the blessings that Isaac blessed you.” Now that angel was Esau’s genius, and he was contesting the blessings.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Hosea 12:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:6:1)


**וַה׳ אֱלֹהֵי הַצְּבָאוֹת ה׳ זִכְרוֹ.** כַּאֲשֶׁר הָיִיתִי מֵאָז כֵּן אֲנִי עַתָּה, וְאִם הֱיִיתֶם הוֹלְכִים עִמִּי בְּתָמִימוּת כְּיַעֲקֹב אֲבִיכֶם (בראשית כה:כז), הָיִיתִי נוֹהֵג עִמָּכֶם כַּאֲשֶׁר נָהַגְתִּי עִמּוֹ:


**And the Lord is the God of the hosts** Just as I was then, so am I now, and had you walked with Me wholeheartedly, like Jacob your father, I would have acted toward you as I acted toward him.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Hosea 12:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:7:1)


**וְאַתָּה בֵּאלֹהֶיךָ תָשׁוּב.** בְּהַבְטָחָתוֹ וּבְמִשְׁעַנְתּוֹ שֶׁהוּא מַבְטִיחֲךָ אַתָּה יָכוֹל לִסְמֹךְ וְתָשׁוּב אֵלָיו, רַק ״חֶסֶד וּמִשְׁפָּט שְׁמֹר״ (מיכה ו:ח) וּמֻבְטָח אַתָּה לְקַוּוֹת לְיִשְׁעוֹ תָּמִיד:


**And you shall return by your God** On His promise and His support, which He promises you, you can rely, and return to Him. Only... **keep loving-kindness and justice** and you may be assured to hope for His salvation always.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Hosea 12:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:8:1)


**כְּנַעַן בְּיָדוֹ מֹאזְנֵי מִרְמָה.** אַתֶּם סְמוּכִים עַל בִּצְעֲכֶם, שֶׁאַתֶּם סוֹחֲרִים וְרַמָּאִים, וְעַל עָשְׁרְכֶם אַתֶּם אוֹמְרִים: ״אַךְ עָשַׁרְתִּי״ (הושע יב:ט), וְלֹא אֶעֱבֹד אֶת הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**A trafficker who has deceitful scales in his hand** You rely on your gain, for you are traffickers and deceivers, and concerning your wealth you say...



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Hosea 12:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:9:1)


**מָצָאתִי אוֹן לִי.** אוֹן – כֹּחַ. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה הָיָה דּוֹרֵשׁ רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן זצ״ל: ״**מָצָאתִי אוֹן לִי**״ – מָצָאתִי לִי שְׁטַר חוֹב שֶׁיֵּשׁ לִי מַלְכוּת עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל, ״וַיֵּלְכוּ גַם אֶחָיו וַיִּפְּלוּ וְגוֹ׳ הִנֶּנּוּ לְךָ לַעֲבָדִים״ (בראשית נ:יח). ״אוֹן לִי״ – דִּינֵי שְׁטָר, כְּמוֹ ״כּוֹתְבִין עָלָיו אוֹנוֹ״, וּכְמוֹ ״בְּאוֹנִי אֶחָד וּבְטִימִי אַחַת״ בִּבְרֵאשִׁית רַבָּה (בראשית רבה ב ב). וְזֶה פֵּרוּשׁוֹ: ״**וַיֹּאמֶר אֶפְרַיִם אַךְ עָשַׁרְתִּי מָצָאתִי אוֹן לִי**״ – יָרָבְעָם בֶּן נְבָט שֶׁהָיָה מִשֵּׁבֶט אֶפְרַיִם מִתְפָּאֵר בְּעַצְמוֹ וְאוֹמֵר: ״**אַךְ עָשַׁרְתִּי, מָצָאתִי אוֹן לִי**״ – שְׁטָר אֶחָד שֶׁכָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל עֲבָדִים לִי, שֶׁאָבִי קָנָה אוֹתָם, דִּכְתִיב: ״וַיֵּלְכוּ גַם אֶחָיו וַיִּפְּלוּ לְפָנָיו וַיֹּאמְרוּ הִנֶּנּוּ לְךָ לַעֲבָדִים״, וּמַה שֶּׁקָּנָה עֶבֶד קָנָה רַבּוֹ, וְכָל מָמוֹנָם שֶׁלִּי. לְפִיכָךְ אֵין בִּי חֵטְא אִם אֶטּוֹל כָּל אֲשֶׁר לָהֶם כִּי הֵם עַבְדַי. מַה כְּתִיב אַחֲרָיו? ״וְאָנֹכִי ה׳ אֱלֹהֶיךָ מֵאֶרֶץ מִצְרַיִם״ (שמות כ:ב) – גְּדֻלָּה הַבָּאָה לְאָבִיךָ בְּמִצְרַיִם מֵאִתִּי הָיְתָה. אָמַר הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא: ״הִנֶּנּוּ לְךָ לַעֲבָדִים״ לֹא שָׁכַחְתָּ, אֲבָל ״אָנֹכִי ה׳ אֱלֹהֶיךָ״ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בַּעֲשֶׂרֶת הַדִּבְּרוֹת שָׁכַחְתָּ, שֶׁהֶעֱמַדְתָּ שְׁנֵי עֲגָלִים, אֶחָד בְּבֵית אֵל וְאֶחָד בְּדָן. וּלְעִנְיַן פְּשׁוּטוֹ שֶׁל מִקְרָא, אַתָּה אוֹמֵר ״**מָצָאתִי אוֹן לִי**״ עַל יְדֵי עֹשֶׁק וּמֹאזְנֵי מִרְמָה. ״וְאָנֹכִי ה׳ אֱלֹהֶיךָ מֵאֶרֶץ מִצְרַיִם״ – שָׁם הִבְחַנְתִּי בֵּין טִפָּה שֶׁל בְּכוֹר לְטִפָּה שֶׁאֵינָהּ שֶׁל בְּכוֹר, אַף אֲנִי הַיּוֹדֵעַ וְנִפְרָע מִמֹּאזְנֵי מִרְמָה הָעֲשׂוּיִין מִבְּלִי הָבִין וּמֵהַטּוֹמֵן מִשְׁקְלוֹתָיו בְּמֶלַח כְּדֵי לְרַמּוֹת.


**Surely I have become rich** Now why should I worship the Holy One, blessed be He? **I have found power for myself** Heb. אוֹן, meaning strength. Rabbi Simon, however, explained it homiletically as follows: I found אוֹן for myself: I found a deed of indebtedness for myself, that I possess kingship over Israel. (Gen. 50:18) “And his brothers too wept and fell etc. ‘Behold we are slaves to you etc.’” אוֹן means the laws of a deed. Comp. (Gittin 43b) “They may write his deed (אוֹנוֹ) for it.” Comp. (Gittin 43b) “They may write his deed אוֹנוֹ for it.” Comp. also (Gen. Rabbah 2:2) With one deed (אוֹנוֹ) and with one price. And this is its explanation: **“And Ephraim said: Surely I have become rich; I have found a deed for myself.”** Jeroboam son of Nebat, who was of the tribe of Ephraim, boasts and declares: Surely I have become rich, I have found a deed for myself, one deed, that all Israel are my slaves, for my father acquired them, as it is written: And his brothers too went and fell before him, and said: Behold we are your slaves. And whatever a slave acquires belongs to his master. Hence, all their property is mine. Therefore, I have no sin if I take all that is theirs, for they are my slaves. What is written after this? And I am the Lord your God from the land of Egypt. The greatness that came to your father in Egypt was from Me. Said the Holy One, blessed be He. ‘Behold we are your slaves,’ you have not forgotten, but ‘I am the Lord your God,’ which was stated in the Decalogue, you have forgotten, for you have erected two calves, one in Bethel and one in Dan. And according to the simple meaning of the verse, you say, “I have found power for myself through oppression and deceitful scales.” And I am the Lord your God from the land of Egypt. There I distinguished between a droplet [of seed] of a firstborn and a droplet which was not of a firstborn. I also know and exact retribution from deceitful scales made without understanding and from one who hides his weights in salt in order to deceive.


###### Rashi on Hosea 12:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:9:2)


**כָּל יְגִיעַי לֹא יִמְצְאוּ לִי.** טוֹב לְךָ אִם אָמַרְתָּ בְּלִבְּךָ: ״כָּל מָמוֹנִי לֹא יְהֵא בּוֹ סִפּוּק לְכַפֵּר עַל עֲוֹנִי אֲשֶׁר חָטָאתִי״. כֵּן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן ״נָבִיא אֲמַר לְהוֹן כָּל עוּתְרֵיכוֹן לָא יִתְקַיֵּם לְכוֹן בְּיוֹם תּוּשְׁלָמַת חוֹבֵיכוֹן״:


**all my toils shall not suffice for my etc.** It would have been better for you had you said to yourself: All my money shall not suffice to atone for my iniquity which I have sinned. In this manner Jonathan renders it: The prophet said to them: All your wealth will not remain with you on the day of the retribution of your sins.


###### Rashi on Hosea 12:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:9:3)


**לֹא יִמְצְאוּ.** לֹא יַסְפִּיקוּ, כְּמוֹ (במדבר יא:כב) ״וּמָצָא לָהֶם״. וְלֹא יִתָּכֵן לְפָרֵשׁ כָּל יְגִיעַי – כָּל הַיְגֵעִים לְבַקֵּשׁ עֲוֹנִי וְלֹא יִמְצְאוּ לִי עָוֹן, שֶׁאִם כֵּן הָיָה רָאוּי לִכְתֹּב כָּל יָגֵעַ בְּלֹא יוֹ״ד וְלִנְקֹד בְּצֵירֵ״י, אֲבָל עַתָּה אֵינוֹ לְשׁוֹן ׳יָגֵעַ׳ אֶלָּא לְשׁוֹן ׳יְגִיעַ׳:


**shall not suffice** Heb. לֹא יִמְצְאוּ, usually translated ‘shall not find.’ They shall not suffice. Comp. (Num. 11:22) “and suffice (וּמָצָא) for them.” But it cannot be explained: כָֹּל-יְגִיעַי, all those who toil to seek my iniquity shall not find any iniquity with me, for, were that the case, it should be spelled יְגֵעַי without a “yud,” and it should be vowelized with a “tseireh,” but now it is not an expression of one who toils but an expression of toil.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Hosea 12:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:10:1)


**עוֹד אוֹשִׁיבְךָ בָאֳהָלִים.** אַכְרִית מִתּוֹכְךָ סוֹחֲרֵי מִרְמָה וְאוֹשִׁיבְךָ בָאֳהָלִים, אַעֲמִיד מִתּוֹכְךָ תַּלְמִידִים עוֹסְקִים בַּתּוֹרָה:


**I will yet make you dwell in tents** I will cut off from your midst deceitful traffickers, and I will cause you to dwell in tents. I will set up from among you students engaging in the Torah.


###### Rashi on Hosea 12:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:10:2)


**כִּימֵי מוֹעֵד.** כִּימֵי מוֹעֵד הָרִאשׁוֹן שֶׁהָיָה ״יַעֲקֹב אִישׁ תָּם יוֹשֵׁב אֹהָלִים״ (בראשית כה:כז):


**as in the days of the [early] times** As in the days of the early times, when Jacob, the unpretentious man, was sitting in tents.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Hosea 12:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:11:1)


**וְדִבַּרְתִּי עַל הַנְּבִיאִים.** לְהוֹכִיחַ אֶתְכֶם וּלְהַחֲזִיר אֶתְכֶם לַמּוּטָב:


**And I spoke to the prophets** to admonish you and to bring you back to do good.


###### Rashi on Hosea 12:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:11:2)


**וּבְיַד הַנְּבִיאִים אֲדַמֶּה.** נִרְאֵיתִי לָהֶם בְּכַמָּה דּוּמְיוֹת (יחזקאל א:כו). דָּבָר אַחֵר: אֲדַמֶּה לָהֶם דְּבָרַי עַל יְדֵי מְשָׁלִים כְּדֵי לְיַשְּׁבָם עַל שׁוֹמְעֵיהֶם:


**and to the prophets I assumed likenesses** I appeared to them in many likenesses. Another explanation. I gave My words likenesses to them through allegories in order to make them comprehensible to their listeners.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Hosea 12:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:12:1)


**אִם גִּלְעָד אָוֶן.** אִם בָּא לָהֶם שֶׁבֶר וְאֹנֶס, הֵם גָּרְמוּ לְעַצְמָם, כִּי אַךְ שָׁוְא הָיוּ, וּבַגִּלְגָּל שְׁוָרִים זִבְּחוּ לַעֲבוֹדָה זָרָה:


**If there is violence in Gilead** If they suffered from destruction and violence, they caused it to themselves, for they were but vanity in Gilgal, and they sacrificed oxen to pagan deities.


###### Rashi on Hosea 12:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:12:2)


**גַּם מִזְבְּחוֹתָם.** רַבִּים כְּגַלִּים שֶׁעַל תַּלְמֵי שָׂדָי, **תַּלְמֵי שָׂדָי** הִיא מַעֲנִית הַמַּחֲרֵשָׁה קְרוּיָה תֶּלֶם:


**also their altars** were as numerous as heaps which are on the furrows of the field. **the furrows of the field** The furrow made by the plowshare is called תֶלֶם.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Hosea 12:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:13:1)


**וַיִּבְרַח יַעֲקֹב שְׂדֵה אֲרָם וְגוֹ׳.** כְּאָדָם שֶׁאוֹמֵר נַחֲזוֹר עַל הָרִאשׁוֹנוֹת שֶׁדִּבַּרְנוּ לְמַעְלָה ״וַיָּשַׂר אֶל מַלְאָךְ״ (הושע יב:ה), וְעוֹד זֹאת עָשִׂיתִי לוֹ הֻצְרַךְ לִבְרֹחַ שְׂדֵה אֲרָם יְדַעְתֶּם אֵיךְ שְׁמַרְתִּיו:


**And Jacob fled to the field of Aram etc.** Like a person who says, “Let us return to the previous topic,” for we spoke above (verse 5): And he strove with an angel, and moreover, I did this for him; when he was forced to flee to the field of Aram, you know how I guarded him.


###### Rashi on Hosea 12:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:13:2)


**וּבְאִשָּׁה שָׁמַר.** אֶת הַצֹּאן:


**and for a wife he guarded** the flocks.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Hosea 12:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:14:1)


**וּבְנָבִיא הֶעֱלָה ה׳ וְגוֹ׳.** וְעַל שֶׁאַתֶּם מְבַזִּין אֶת הַנְּבִיאִים וּמַלְעִיגִין בְּדִבְרֵיהֶם (דברי הימים ב לו:טז), הֲלֹא **בְּנָבִיא הֶעֱלָה וְגוֹ׳**:


**And, through a prophet, the Lord brought up etc.** And, insofar as you disgrace the prophets and mock their words, is it not so that through a prophet God brought us etc.?



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Hosea 12:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:15:1)


**הִכְעִיס אֶפְרַיִם.** לְבוֹרְאוֹ, לְבַזּוֹת אֶת נְבִיאָיו וְלִבְגֹּד בּוֹ:


**Ephraim provoked** his Creator by despising His prophets and betraying Him.


###### Rashi on Hosea 12:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:15:2)


**תַּמְרוּרִים.** הֵם לוֹ, כִּי דָּמָיו אֲשֶׁר שָׁפַךְ בְּהַחֲטִיאוֹ אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל לְהִצָּמֵד לַעֲבוֹדָה זָרָה, וְהַמַּחֲטִיאוֹ לָאָדָם קָשֶׁה לוֹ מִן הַהוֹרְגוֹ, כַּאֲשֶׁר אָנוּ לְמֵדִין מֵעַמּוֹן וּמוֹאָב שֶׁהִטְעוּ אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל לְהִצָּמֵד לְבַעַל פְּעוֹר (במדבר כה:ג), וְעָנְשָׁן הַכָּתוּב יוֹתֵר מִמִּצְרִי וַאֲדוֹמִי שֶׁטְּבָעוּם בַּנָּהָר וְיָצְאוּ בַּחֶרֶב לִקְרָאתָם (ספרי דברים רנב:ג):


**yea, bitterness** (Heb. תַּמְרוּרִים,) they are to him for his blood which he shed by causing Israel to sin by clinging to idolatry and one who causes a person to sin is worse to him than one who kills him, as we learn from Ammon and Moab, who misled Israel to cling to Baal-Peor, and Scripture punished them more severely than an Egyptian and an Edomite, who drowned them in the river and went forth toward them with the sword...


###### Rashi on Hosea 12:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:15:3)


**עָלָיו יִטּוֹשׁ.** עָלָיו יַטִּילֵם הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא.


**shall be cast upon him** The Holy One, blessed be He, shall cast it upon him.


###### Rashi on Hosea 12:15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:15:4)


**וְחֶרְפָּתוֹ.** שֶׁחֵרֵף אֶת שְׁלֹמֹה, כָּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (מלכים א יא:כו): ״וַיָּרֶם יָד בַּמֶּלֶךְ״, שֶׁהוֹכִיחוֹ בָּרַבִּים עַל אֲשֶׁר בָּנָה שְׁלֹמֹה אֶת הַמִּלּוֹא, כְּדִמְפֹרָשׁ בְּחֵלֶק (סנהדרין קא, ב):


**and his disgrace** which he disgraced Solomon, as the matter is stated: (I Kings 11:26) “He raised his hand against the king,” that he rebuked him in public because Solomon had built up the Millo, as is explained in [the chapter entitled] Chelek (San. 101b).


###### Rashi on Hosea 12:15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/12:15:5)


**יָשִׁיב לוֹ אֲדֹנָיו.** הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, שֶׁהֲרֵי הוּא הֵרַע לַעֲשׂוֹת יוֹתֵר מִמֶּנּוּ (הושע א:ד):


**his Lord shall requite upon him** I.e., the Holy One, blessed be He, since he surpassed him in his evil deeds.



## Chapter 13


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Hosea 13:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:1:1)


**כְּדַבֵּר אֶפְרַיִם רְתֵת.** כְּשֶׁקִּנֵּא יָרָבְעָם לַמָּקוֹם וְדִבֵּר כְּנֶגֶד שְׁלֹמֹה דְּבָרִים קָשִׁים וּבִרְעָדָה (סנהדרין קא ב), שֶׁהֲרֵי מֶלֶךְ גָּדוֹל הָיָה שְׁלֹמֹה:


**When Ephraim spoke with trembling** When Jeroboam showed zeal for the Omnipresent and spoke harshly against Solomon, and tremblingly, for Solomon was a great king.


###### Rashi on Hosea 13:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:1:2)


**נָשָׂא הוּא בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל.** מִשָּׁם זָכָה לְהִנָּשֵׂא לִהְיוֹת מֶלֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל (מלכים א יב:כ):


**he became exalted in Israel** Thence he merited to be elevated to he king over Israel.


###### Rashi on Hosea 13:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:1:3)


**וַיֶּאְשַׁם בַּבַּעַל.** כֵּיוָן שֶׁעָלָה לִגְדֻלָּה, וַיֶּאְשַׁם בַּעֲבוֹדָה זָרָה:


**when he became guilty through Baal** As soon as he assumed greatness and became guilty with Baal.


###### Rashi on Hosea 13:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:1:4)


**וַיָּמֹת.** נִכְרַת בֵּית יָרָבְעָם (מלכים א יד:י), וְכֵן בֵּית אַחְאָב (מלכים א כא:כא). וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״כַּד מְמַלֵּל חַד מִדְבֵית אֶפְרַיִם רְתִיתָא אָחִיד לְהוֹן לְעַמְמַיָּא, מִתְרַבְרְבִין הֲווֹ בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל, וְכַדּוּ דְּחָבוּ בְּפוּלְחַן טָעוּתָא מִתְקַטְּלִין״:


**he died** i.e., Jeroboam’s dynasty was terminated, and so was Ahab’s dynasty. Jonathan renders: When one of the house of Ephraim would speak, fear would seize the nations. They were great among Israel, but when they sinned by worshipping idols, they were slain.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Hosea 13:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:2:1)


**עַתָּה.** בֵּית יֵהוּא שֶׁרָאוּ כָּל זֹאת יוֹסִיפוּ לַחֲטֹא:


**And now** Jehu’s dynasty, who saw all this, continue to sin.


###### Rashi on Hosea 13:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:2:2)


**כִּתְבוּנָם.** כְּתַבְנִיתָם:


**according to their pattern** Heb. כִּתְבוּנָם.


###### Rashi on Hosea 13:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:2:3)


**זוֹבְחֵי אָדָם עֲגָלִים יִשָּׁקוּן.** כֹּמְרֵי הַמֹּלֶךְ אוֹמְרִים לְיִשְׂרָאֵל מִי שֶׁזּוֹבֵחַ בְּנוֹ לַעֲבוֹדָה זָרָה, כְּדַאי הוּא לִהְיוֹת נוֹשֵׁק הָעֵגֶל, שֶׁהֲרֵי דּוֹרוֹן חָבִיב הִקְרִיב לוֹ. כָּךְ פֵּרְשׁוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ בְּסַנְהֶדְרִין (סנהדרין סג, ב). וּמְיֻשָּׁב הוּא עַל לְשׁוֹן הַמִּקְרָא יוֹתֵר מִתַּרְגּוּם שֶׁל יוֹנָתָן:


**Those who sacrifice man may kiss the calves** The priests of Molech say to Israel, “Whoever sacrifices his son to the idol is worthy of kissing the calf” for he has offered him a precious gift. So did our Sages explain this in Sanhedrin (63b), and it fits the wording of the verse better than Jonathan’s translation.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Hosea 13:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:3:1)


**וְכַטַּל מַשְׁכִּים הוֹלֵךְ.** ״וּכְטַלָא דְּמוֹהִי פָּסִיק״ (תרגום יונתן):


**and like dew that passes away early** Jonathan renders: And like dew, the water of which evaporates.


###### Rashi on Hosea 13:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:3:2)


**כְּמוֹץ יְסוֹעַר מִגֹּרֶן.** ״כְּמוֹצָא דְּנָשְׁבָה רוּחַ מֵאִידְרָא״:


**like chaff that is driven with the wind out of the threshingfloor** Jonathan renders: Like chaff which the wind blows from the threshingfloor.


###### Rashi on Hosea 13:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:3:3)


**יְסֹעַר.** אֲשֶׁר סְעָרָה תִּשָּׂאֶנּוּ:


{e wind lifts up.}



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Hosea 13:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:4:1)


**וְאָנֹכִי ה׳ אֱלֹהֶיךָ.** וְלֹא הָיָה לְךָ לִמְרוֹד בִּי:


**And I am the Lord your God** and you should not have rebelled against Me



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Hosea 13:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:5:1)


**אֲנִי יְדַעְתִּיךָ.** נָתַתִּי לֵב לָדַעַת צָרְכְּךָ וְסִפַּקְתִּיךָ:


**I knew you** I set My heart to know your necessity, and I provided you.


###### Rashi on Hosea 13:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:5:2)


**תַּלְאוּבוֹת.** אֵין לוֹ דִּמְיוֹן, וּפִתְרוֹנוֹ לְפִי עִנְיָנוֹ: תֵּל שֶׁאוֹבִין בּוֹ כָּל טוֹבָה וְאֵין מוֹצְאִין:


**deprivation** Heb. תַלְאוּבֹת. There is no similarity to this in Scripture, and its interpretation is, according to the context, תֵּל, a heap, שֶאוֹבִין, where they long for all good and do not find it.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Hosea 13:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:6:1)


**כְּמַרְעִיתָם.** כַּאֲשֶׁר בָּאוּ לְאֶרֶץ מַרְעִיתָם וְהָיוּ שְׂבֵעִים, אָז:


**When they grazed** When they entered the land of their pasture and they were sated, then...


###### Rashi on Hosea 13:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:6:2)


**שָׂבְעוּ וַיָּרָם לִבָּם.** כְּמוֹ ״וַיָּרֶם בַּמַּטֶּה״ (שמות ז:כ), לְשׁוֹן ׳מֵרִים׳. דָּבָר אַחֵר: וַיָּרָם בְּכָאן וַיִּגְבַּהּ הוּא עַצְמוֹ:


**they were sated and their heart became haughty** (Ex. 7:20) “And he lifted (וַיָרֶם) the staff,” is an expression of lifting something else. “Became haughty (וַיָרָם)” here means that he himself was lifted.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Hosea 13:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:7:1)


**עַל דֶּרֶךְ אָשׁוּר.** כָּל ׳אַשּׁוּר׳ שֶׁבַּמִּקְרָא דָּגֵשׁ, וְזֶה רָפֵי, שֶׁאֵינוֹ שֵׁם מָקוֹם אֶלָּא אֶאֱרוֹב וְאֶשְׁקוֹד, כְּמוֹ ״אֲשׁוּרֶנּוּ וְלֹא קָרוֹב״ (במדבר כד:יז):


**by the way I will lurk** Heb. אָשּׁוּר. Every instance of אָשּׁוּר in Scripture is punctuated with a “dagesh,” but this one is “weak,” since it is not a place name but it means, “I will lurk and ambush.” Comp. (Num. 24:17) “I see him (אֲשּׁוּרֶנוּ) but not near.”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Hosea 13:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:8:1)


**כְּדֹב שַׁכּוּל.** כְּמוֹ ׳שׁוֹכֵל׳, כַּאֲשֶׁר תֹּאמַר ׳חַנּוּן׳ וְ׳רַחוּם׳, כָּךְ שַׁכּוּל. כְּלוֹמַר, כֻּלּוֹ לָבוּשׁ שִׁכּוּלִים וּמוּכָן לְשַׁכֵּל אֲנָשִׁים:


**as a bereaving bear** Heb. שַׁכּוּל. Like שּׁוֹכֵל, as you say חָנּוּן, gracious, and רַחוּם, merciful, so שַּׁכּוּל, i.e., entirely attired with bereavements and ready to bereave people.


###### Rashi on Hosea 13:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:8:2)


**וְאֶקְרַע סְגוֹר לִבָּם.** כְּדֶרֶךְ הַדֹּב אוֹחֵז צִפָּרְנָיו בֶּחָזֶה וְקוֹרֵעַ עַד הַלֵּב. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: **סְגוֹר לִבָּם** – אֶת לִבָּם הַסָּגוּר מִלְּהָבִין לָשׁוּב אֵלַי:


**and I will rend the enclosure of their heart** like the habit of the bear, which holds its nails on the chest and rends until the heart. Another explanation: **the enclosure of the heart** Their heart which is closed from understanding to return to Me.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Hosea 13:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:9:1)


**שִׁחֶתְךָ יִשְׂרָאֵל.** חִבַּלְתָּ עַצְמְךָ יִשְׂרָאֵל, **כִּי בִי** פָּשַׁעְתָּ, מָרַדְתָּ **בְעֶזְרֶךָ**, וּמִקְרָא קָצָר הוּא זֶה, וְהַמֵּבִין בִּלְשׁוֹן הַמִּקְרָא מְיֻשָּׁב הוּא עַל הַלֵּב:


**You have destroyed yourself, O Israel** Heb. שִּׁחֶתְךָ.


###### Rashi on Hosea 13:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:9:2)


**כִּי בִי.** כִּי בִי הָיָה הַמֶּרֶד אֲשֶׁר מָרַדְתָּ, וְאִם תֹּאמַר מַה אִכְפַּת לְךָ? **בְעֶזְרֶךָ** מָרַדְתָּ כְּשֶׁמָּרַדְתָּ בִי:


**for against Me** you have rebelled: you have rebelled against your help. This is an ellipsis, but [to] one who understands the language of the Scriptures, it makes sense. For against Me was the rebellion which you rebelled, and if you ask, “What does it mean to You?” Against your help you rebelled when you rebelled against Me.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Hosea 13:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:10:1)


**אֱהִי מַלְכְּךָ אֵפוֹא.** יוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״אַיֵּה מַלְכָּךְ״. וַאֲנִי אוֹמֵר, אֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ לְעָקְרוֹ מִמַּשְׁמָעוּתוֹ. ״אֱהִי״ – עוֹמֵד מִנֶּגֶד לִרְאוֹת ״אֵפוֹא מַלְכְּךָ״, שֶׁאֶעֱשֶׂה עַצְמִי רוֹאֶה מָה אַחֲרִיתְךָ. ״אֵפֹא מוֹשִׁיעֶךָ״.


**I will be, where is your king?** Heb. אֱהִי מַלְכְּךָ אֵפוֹא. Jonathan renders: Where is your king? But I say that it is unnecessary to interpret it other than its apparent meaning. I will be standing from afar to see where your king is, for I will make Myself see what your end will be, where your savior is.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Hosea 13:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:12:1)


**צָרוּר עֲוֹן אֶפְרַיִם.** לֹא וִתַּרְתִּי עָלָיו, ״צָפוּן הוּא אִתִּי״ (דברים לב:לד):


**The iniquity of Ephraim is bound up** I did not overlook it; it is laid up with Me.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Hosea 13:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:13:1)


**כִּי עֵת.** צָרָה בָּאָה לוֹ, אֲשֶׁר לֹא יוּכַל לַעֲמֹד וּלְהִתְקַיֵּם:


**for [it is] a time** For a time of trouble has come upon him, from which he will not be able to stand and endure.


###### Rashi on Hosea 13:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:13:2)


**בְּמִשְׁבַּר בָּנִים.** שֶׁהַיּוֹלֶדֶת יוֹשֶׁבֶת עָלָיו לֵילֵד, וּבְלַעַ״ז קוֹרִין לְמַשְׁבַּר שיל״א:


**in the birthstool of the children** upon which the woman in confinement sits to give birth. And in Old French they call a birthstool sele.


###### Rashi on Hosea 13:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:13:3)


**בְּמִשְׁבַּר בָּנִים.** כְּמַשְׁבֵּר הֶעָשׂוּי לְלֵידַת בָּנִים:


**in the birthstool of the children** i.e., in the birthstool made for the birth of children.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Hosea 13:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:14:1)


**מִיַּד שְׁאוֹל אֶפְדֵּם.** אֲנִי הוּא שֶׁהָיִיתִי פּוֹדָם מִיַּד שְׁאוֹל וְגוֹאֲלָם מִמָּוֶת, אֲבָל עַתָּה **אֱהִי דְבָרֶיךָ מָוֶת** אָשִׂים עַצְמִי לְדַבֵּר עָלֶיךָ דִּבְרֵי מָוֶת:


**From the clutches of the grave I would ransom them** I am He Who would ransom them from the clutches of the grave and redeem them from death, but now... **I will be your words of death** Heb. אֱהִי דְבָרֶיךָ. I will place Myself to speak of you words of death.


###### Rashi on Hosea 13:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:14:2)


**אֱהִי.** קוֹטֵב עָלֶיךָ גְּזֵרַת **שְׁאוֹל**:


**I will decree...upon you** Heb. קָטָבְךָ. I will decree upon you the decree of the grave.


###### Rashi on Hosea 13:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:14:3)


**נֹחַם יִסָּתֵר מֵעֵינָי.** לֹא אֶנָּחֵם עַל הָרָעָה הַזֹּאת. ׳נֹחַם׳ לְשׁוֹן ׳נִחוּם׳ וְהַמֵּ״ם מִשֹּׁרֶשׁ הַתֵּבָה, כְּמוֹ מֵ״ם שֶׁל ״נֹעַם״, שֶׁהֲרֵי הַטַּעַם לְמַעְלָה וְהַחֵי״ת נָקוּד פַּתַּח, וְאִם הָיָה לְשׁוֹן ׳נָח׳ וְהַמֵּ״ם מְשַׁמֵּשׁ בּוֹ לְשׁוֹן רַבִּים, הָיָה טַעְמוֹ תַּחַת הַחֵי״ת וּנְקוּדָה קָמַץ, ״נֹחָם״ (משלי כ:כט), ״רוּחָם״ (שופטים ח:ג).


**Remorse shall be hidden from My eyes** Heb. נֹחַם. I will not regret this evil. נֹחֵם is an expression of regret (נִחוּם), and the “mem” is a radical like the “mem” of נֹעַם, pleasantness, for the accent is on the first syllable, and the “heth” is vowelized with a “pattah.” Were it an expression of resting (נָח), with the “mem” serving as a suffix denoting the plural, the accent would be under the “heth,” and it would be vowelized with a “kamatz” [like כֹּחָם their strength; רוּחָם, their spirit.]



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Hosea 13:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:15:1)


**כִּי הוּא בֵּין אַחִים יַפְרִיא.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״אֲרֵי אִינוּן מִתְקַרְיָין בְּנִין וְעוֹבָדִין מְקַלְקְלִין אַסְגִּיאוּ״. אַחִים – לְשׁוֹן ׳דָּבָר רַע׳, כְּמוֹ (יחזקאל כא:כ) ״אֶת עֲשׂוּיָה לַבָּרָק״, ״וֶאֱמָר אָח אֶל כָּל תּוֹעֲבוֹת״ (יחזקאל ו:יא).


**For though he flourish among the marshes** Heb. כִּי הוּא בֵּין אַחִים יַפְרִיא. Jonathan renders: For they are called sons, but they increased corrupt deeds. אַחִים An expression of an evil thing. Comp. (Ezekiel 21:20) “Woe! (אָח) It is made for a blade.” Also (ibid. 6:11) “And say, ‘Woe!’ (אָח) to all the... abominations.” יַפְרִיא An expression similar to (Deut. 29:17) פֹּרֶה רֹאשׁ וְלַעֲנָה, that hears (פֹּרַה) hemlock and wormwood.” But I say that אַחִים is an expression similar to (Gen. 41:2) “And they grazed in the marsh (בָּאָחוּ).”


###### Rashi on Hosea 13:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:15:2)


**יַפְרִיא.** לְשׁוֹן ״פּוֹרֶה רֹאשׁ וְלַעֲנָה״ (דברים כט:יז). וַאֲנִי אוֹמֵר ׳אַחִים׳ לְשׁוֹן ״וַתִּרְעֶינָה בָאָחוּ״ (בראשית מא:ב), בֵּין **אַחִים יַפְרִיא** – מַפְרִיחַ וְצוֹמֵחַ בָּאָחוּ בֵּין אַחִים, בֵּין הַגָּדֵל בְּפַלְגֵי מַיִם אֲשֶׁר תָּמִיד יַפְרִיא. וּלְפִי שֶׁדִּמָּהוּ לְאָחוּ הוּא אוֹמֵר **יָבֹא קָדִים וְיֵבוֹשׁ מְקוֹרוֹ**. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **כִּי הוּא בֵּין אַחִים יַפְרִיא** – יָרָבְעָם הוּא בֵּן שֶׁהִפְרִיא אַחֲוָתָן שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל, עַל יָדוֹ נֶחְלְקוּ לִשְׁנֵי מַמְלָכוֹת. יַפְרִיא לְשׁוֹן ״פֶּרֶא אָדָם״ (בראשית טז:יב).


**For though he flourish among the marshes** Heb. יַפְרִיא. Blossoms and grows in the marsh. Among the marshes, among those growing in the marshes, which always flourish. And, since He compares him to a marsh, He says: An east wind shall come... and his spring shall dry up. Another explanation: For he decreased among the brothers, i.e., Jeroboam. He was a son who decreased the brotherhood [other versions: the heritage] of Israel. Through him, they were divided into two kingdoms. Decreased יַפְרִיע. An expression similar to (Gen. 16:12) “One who decreases men (פֶּרֶא אָדָם) .”


###### Rashi on Hosea 13:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:15:3)


**יָבוֹא קָדִים.** ״מֶלֶךְ תַּקִּיף כְּרוּחַ קִידוּמָא בְּמֵימְרָא דַּה׳ מֵאוֹרַח מִדְבְּרָא יִסַּק״:


**an east wind shall come** A king, strong as the east wind, shall come up by the word of the Lord from the way of the desert.


###### Rashi on Hosea 13:15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/13:15:4)


**הוּא יִשְׁסֶה.** אוֹתוֹ הַמֶּלֶךְ יִשְׁסֶה אוֹצְרוֹת כָּל כְּלִי חֶמְדָּתוֹ:


**he shall plunder** That king shall plunder the treasures of all coveted vessels.



## Chapter 14


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Hosea 14:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:1:1)


**תֵּאָשַׁם שׁוֹמְרוֹן.** מֵעַתָּה תִּגָּלֶה אַשְׁמָתָהּ:


**Samaria shall be accounted guilty** From now on, her guilt will be revealed.


###### Rashi on Hosea 14:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:1:2)


**הָרִיּוֹתָיו.** נָשִׁים מְעֻבָּרוֹת שֶׁבְּתוֹכָהּ:


**and their pregnant women** Heb. וְהָריּוֹתָיו. The pregnant women in its midst.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Hosea 14:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:2:1)


**שׁוּבָה יִשְׂרָאֵל.** שֶׁבְּאֶרֶץ יְהוּדָה, פֶּן יִקְרֶה אֶתְכֶם כְּשׁוֹמְרוֹן. לְכָךְ נִסְמְכוּ הָעִנְיָנִים. מָשָׁל לְמֶלֶךְ שֶׁמָּרְדָה עָלָיו מְדִינָה, שָׁלַח הַמֶּלֶךְ פּוֹלִימַרְכוֹס וְאָמַר לְהַחֲרִיבָהּ. הָיָה אוֹתוֹ פּוֹלִימַרְכוֹס בָּקִי וּמְיֻשָּׁב, אָמַר לָהֶם: טְלוּ לָכֶם יָמִים, וְאִם לָאו אֲנִי עוֹשֶׂה לָכֶם כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁעָשִׂיתִי לִמְדִינָה פְּלוֹנִית וּלְחַבְרוֹתֶיהָ, וּלְאִיפַּרְכִיָּא פְּלוֹנִית וּלְחַבְרוֹתֶיהָ. לְכָךְ נֶאֱמַר: ״תֶּאְשַׁם שׁוֹמְרוֹן״ (הושע יג:טז), וְאוֹמֵר: ״שׁוּבָה יִשְׂרָאֵל עַד ה׳ אֱלֹהֶיךָ״ כִּדְאִיתָא בְּסִפְרִי בְּפָרָשַׁת וַיֵּשֶׁב יִשְׂרָאֵל בַּשִּׁטִּים:


**Return, O Israel** You, who are in the land of Judah, lest what happens to Samaria happens to you. Therefore, the topics are juxtaposed. This can be compared to a king against whom a province rebelled. The king sent a general and commanded him to destroy it. That general was expert and deliberate. He said to them, “Take for yourselves days (sic); otherwise, I will do to you as I have done to such-and-such a province and to its allies, and to such-and-such a prefecture and to its allies.” Therefore it says, “Samaria shall be accounted guilty,” and then Scripture says: “Return, O Israel.” As is found in Sifrei in the section commencing. (Num. 25:1), “And Israel abode in Shittim.”


###### Rashi on Hosea 14:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:2:2)


**עַד ה׳ אֱלֹהֶיךָ.** תָּנֵי בְּשֵׁם רַבִּי מֵאִיר (פסיקתא דרב כהנא כד,יג): שׁוּבָה יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּעוֹד שֶׁהוּא ה׳ בְּמִדַּת הָרַחֲמִים, וְאִם לָאו – אֱלֹהֶיךָ מִדַּת הַדִּין, עַד דְּלָא יִתְעֲבֵד סַנֵּגוֹרְיָא קַטֵּגוֹרְיָא:


**to the Lord your God** One taught in the name of Rabbi Meir: Return, O Israel, while He is still יהוה, with the Divine Attribute of Mercy; otherwise, He is אֶלֹהֶיךָ with the Divine Attribute of Justice, before the defense becomes the prosecution. [from Pesikta d’Rav Kahana, p. 164a]


###### Rashi on Hosea 14:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:2:3)


**כִּי כָשַׁלְתָּ.** בָּאוּ לְךָ מִכְשׁוֹלִים בְּעַד עֲוֹנֶיךָ:


**for you have stumbled in your iniquity** Obstacles have come to you because of your iniquity.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Hosea 14:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:3:1)


**כָּל תִּשָּׂא עָוֹן.** כָּל עֲוֹנוֹתֵינוּ סְלַח:


**You shall forgive all iniquity** Heb. כָּל-תִּשָׂא עָוֹן. Forgive all our iniquities.


###### Rashi on Hosea 14:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:3:2)


**וְקַח טוֹב.** וְלַמְּדֵנוּ דֶּרֶךְ טוֹב. דָּבָר אַחֵר: מְעַט מַעֲשִׂים טוֹבִים שֶׁבְּיָדֵינוּ קַח בְּיָדְךָ וְשָׁפְטֵנוּ אַחֲרֵיהֶם, וְכֵן דָּוִד אוֹמֵר ״מִלְּפָנֶיךָ מִשְׁפָּטִי יֵצֵא עֵינֶיךָ תֶּחֱזֶינָה מֵישָׁרִים״ (תהלים יז:ב). דָּבָר אַחֵר: **וְקַח טוֹב** – וְקַבֵּל הוֹדָיָה מֵאִתָּנוּ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״טוֹב לְהֹדוֹת לַה׳״ (תהלים צב:ב):


**and teach [us the] good [way]** Heb. וְקַח-טוֹב. And teach us the good way. Another explanation: The few good deeds in our hands take in Your hand and judge us accordingly. And so does David say (Psalms 17:2): “Let my sentence come forth from before You, may Your eyes behold the right.” Another explanation: And accept good And accept confession from us, as it is said (Psalms 92:2): “It is good to confess to the Lord.”


###### Rashi on Hosea 14:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:3:3)


**וּנְשַׁלְּמָה פָרִים.** שֶׁהָיָה לָנוּ לְהַקְרִיב לְפָנֶיךָ, נְשַׁלֵּם אוֹתָם בְּרִצּוּי דִּבְרֵי **שְׂפָתֵינוּ** (יומא פו, ב):


**and let us render [for] bulls** that we should have sacrificed before you, let us render them with the placation of the words of our lips.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Hosea 14:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:4:1)


**אַשּׁוּר לֹא יוֹשִׁיעֵנוּ.** עוֹד זֹאת אָמְרוּ לְפָנָיו: לֹא נְבַקֵּשׁ עוֹד עֶזְרַת אָדָם, לֹא מֵאַשּׁוּר וְלֹא מִמִּצְרָיִם:


**Assyria shall not save us** Say this also before Him, “We no longer seek the aid of man, neither from Assyria nor from Egypt.”


###### Rashi on Hosea 14:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:4:2)


**עַל סוּס לֹא נִרְכָּב.** זוֹ הִיא עֶזְרַת מִצְרַיִם, שֶׁהָיוּ שׁוֹלְחִים לָהֶם סוּסִים, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמְרוּ לִישַׁעְיָה (ישעיה ל:טז) ״לֹא כִי עַל סוּס נָנוּס וְעַל קַל נִרְכָּב״: **וְלֹא נֹאמַר עוֹד**:


**we will not ride on horses** This is the aid from Egypt, who would send them horses, as they said to Isaiah (30:16), “No, but on horses will we flee... And on swift steeds will we ride.”


###### Rashi on Hosea 14:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:4:3)


**לְמַעֲשֵׂה יָדֵינוּ.** שֶׁהֵם אֱלֹהֵינוּ:


**nor will we say any longer** to the work of our hands that they are our gods.


###### Rashi on Hosea 14:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:4:4)


**אֲשֶׁר בְּךָ.** לְבַדְּךָ תִּהְיֶה תִּקְוָתֵנוּ, הַמְרַחֵם יְתוֹמִים:


**for in You** alone shall our hope be, You Who grant mercy to the orphans.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Hosea 14:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:5:1)


**אֶרְפָּא מְשׁוּבָתָם.** אָמַר הַנָּבִיא: כָּךְ אָמַר לִי רוּחַ הַקֹּדֶשׁ, מֵאַחַר שֶׁיֹּאמְרוּ לְפָנַי כֵּן, אֶרְפָּא מְשׁוּבָתָם וְאֹהֲבֵם בְּנִדְבַת רוּחִי, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינָן רְאוּיִין לְאַהֲבָה, אֶתְנַדֵּב לְאַהֲבָתָם, **כִּי שָׁב אַפִּי מִמֶּנּוּ** (תנחומא, עקב ד):


**I will remedy their backsliding** Said the prophet: So has the Holy Spirit said to me. After they say this before Me, I will remedy their backsliding, and I will love them with My charitable spirit. Although they do not deserve the love, I will love them charitably since My wrath has turned away from them.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Hosea 14:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:6:1)


**וְיַךְ.** הַטַּל אֶת שָׁרָשָׁיו וְיַצְלִיחֵם. **כַּלְּבָנוֹן** – כְּשָׁרְשֵׁי אִילָנֵי הַלְּבָנוֹן שֶׁהֵם גְּדוֹלִים:


**and it shall strike** I.e. the dew shall strike its roots and cause them to prosper **like the Lebanon** like the roots of the trees of the Lebanon, which are large.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Hosea 14:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:7:1)


**יֵלְכוּ יוֹנְקוֹתָיו.** ״יִסְגּוֹן בְּנִין וּבְנָן, וִיהִי כְּזִיו מְנָרְתָא דְקוּדְשָׁא זִיוְהוֹן וְרֵיחֲהוֹן כְּרֵיחַ קְטֹרֶת בּוּסְמַיָּא״:


**Its branches shall go forth** Sons and daughters shall increase **and it shall be** Their beauty shall be like the beauty of the menorah of the Temple, and their fragrance like the fragrance of the incense.


###### Rashi on Hosea 14:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:7:2)


**כַּלְּבָנוֹן.** כְּבֵית מַקְדְּשָׁא:


**like the Lebanon** Like the Temple.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Hosea 14:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:8:1)


**יָשׁוּבוּ יֹשְׁבֵי בְצִלּוֹ.** אוֹתָן שֶׁהָיוּ כְּבָר יוֹשְׁבִים בְּצֵל הַלְּבָנוֹן, שֶׁדִּמָּה בּוֹ יִשְׂרָאֵל וּבֵית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ (הושע ו:ז), וְעַכְשָׁיו גָּלוּ מֵעִמּוֹ, יָשׁוּבוּ אֵלָיו:


**Those who dwelt in its shade shall return** Those who already dwelt in the shade of the Lebanon, to which He compared Israel and the Temple, and now were exiled there from, shall return to it.


###### Rashi on Hosea 14:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:8:2)


**זִכְרוֹ כְּיֵין לְבָנוֹן.** ״כְּדוּכְרָן יַבְבוֹת חֲצוֹצְרָתָא דְּעַל חֲמַר עַתִּיק דְּמִתְנַסֵּךְ בְּבֵית מַקְדְּשָׁא״, שֶׁהָיוּ תּוֹקְעִין בַּחֲצוֹצְרוֹת עַל הַנְּסָכִים כְּשֶׁהַלְוִיִּם אוֹמְרִים בַּשִּׁיר:


**its fragrance shall be like the wine of Lebanon** Jonathan renders: Like the remembrance of the blasts of the trumpets over the old wine poured for libations in the Temple. For they would blow the trumpets over the libations when the Levites would recite the song.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Hosea 14:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:9:1)


**אֶפְרַיִם.** יֹאמַר: ״מַה לִּי עוֹד לָלֶכֶת אַחֲרֵי הָעֲצַבִּים?״ וְיָשׁוּבוּ מֵעֲבוֹדָה זָרָה. (מלכים א יב:כח-כט) **אֲנִי אֶעֱנֶהוּ** מִצָּרָתוֹ:


**Ephraim** will say, “What more do I need to follow the images?” And they will turn away from idolatry. **I will answer him** I will answer him from his trouble.


###### Rashi on Hosea 14:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:9:2)


**וַאֲשׁוּרֶנּוּ.** אֶרְאֶה בְּעָנְיוֹ:


**and I will look upon him** I will look upon his affliction.


###### Rashi on Hosea 14:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:9:3)


**אֲנִי כִּבְרוֹשׁ רַעֲנָן.** אֲנִי לוֹ נִכְפָּף לִהְיוֹת אוֹחֵז יָדוֹ בִּי, כִּבְרוֹשׁ רַעֲנָן הַנִּכְפָּף לָאָרֶץ שֶׁאָדָם אוֹחֵז בַּעֲנָפָיו, כְּלוֹמַר אֶהְיֶה מָצוּי לוֹ:


**I am like a leafy cypress tree** I will bend down for him to hold his hand on Me as the leafy cypress which is bent down to the ground, which a man holds by its branches; i.e., I will be accessible to him.


###### Rashi on Hosea 14:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:9:4)


**מִמֶּנִּי פֶּרְיְךָ נִמְצָא.** וַהֲלֹא אֲנִי הוּא שֶׁכָּל טוֹבָתְךָ מֵאִתִּי בָּאָה:


**from Me your fruit is found** Am I not He? For all your good emanates from Me.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Hosea 14:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:10:1)


**מִי חָכָם.** בָּכֶם **וְיָבֵן אֵלֶּה.** מִי חָכָם בָּכֶם וְיִתְבּוֹנֵן לָתֵת לֵב לְכָל אֵלֶּה וְיָשׁוּב אֵלַי:


**Who is wise and will understand these** Who among you is wise and will ponder to put his heart to all these and return to Me?


###### Rashi on Hosea 14:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Hosea/r/14:10:2)


**וּפוֹשְׁעִים יִכָּשְׁלוּ בָם.** בִּשְׁבִילָם, עַל שֶׁלֹּא הָלְכוּ בָּהֶן. כֵּן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן:


**and the rebellious shall stumble on them** i.e., because of them, because they did not walk in them. Jonathan renders in this manner.
